## Review

**9 out of 10, Excellent.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67. Each page carries one idea, with one accent color, one typeface and consistent titles, and the visuals do the explaining: the cell animation on slides 17 to 22, the wet versus dry electrode comparison on slides 32 and 34, and the blocks on slides 50 and 51, where the empty space in the dry-process column makes the point. Contrast is strong on black, and the readable slides show good hierarchy and alignment. Small caption text on some diagrams (slides 6, 67) is only fit for a large screen, and a few pages carry little content beyond a photo and a stat, but this suits a live keynote with narration.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the progress ring and tile tracker, the single red accent against black, the animated cell build on slides 17 to 22, and the sparse before and after process diagrams on slides 50 and 51.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the tiny labels, axis text and source lines on chart and diagram slides such as 6, 10, 24 and 67, and fix the chart on slide 10, which has no value labels.

**What would change the score:** I would label the key values on the line charts (slides 10, 11, 68) and make the slide 67 waterfall bars carry more of the color emphasis so the totals read faster.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 74 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a September 22, 2020 title slide and a climate framing. A CO2 concentration chart leads to a note that the shift to sustainable energy needs more affordable EVs and energy storage, built faster and with less investment. It then sets goals: terawatt-hour scale battery production and more affordable cells. Bar charts of gigawatt-hour demand by vehicle segment and by heating and electricity category show the scale of growth required. The middle sections cover cell design, the cell factory, anode materials, cathode materials, and cell-vehicle integration, each with a cost-per-kWh reduction figure. Topics include larger cylindrical cells, a dry electrode process, silicon anodes, a diversified cathode approach, North American cathode and lithium supply, recycling, giga casting, and structural batteries. The deck closes with a 2030 target of 3 TWh, a stacked chart of cost benefits from vertical integration, an industry cost trend compared with Tesla's forecast, a call for suppliers to help, and a closing look at lower-cost vehicles and a high-performance model.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Tesla battery day presentation deck have?**

The deck has 74 slides. Slide 1 is the title slide dated September 22, 2020.

**What battery production targets does the Tesla deck set out?**

Slide 39 gives the plan as 100 GWh in 2022 and 3 TWh by 2030. Slide 6 is titled Goal One: Terawatt-hour Scale Battery Production.

**What does the Tesla deck say about silicon anodes and their cost?**

Slide 41 says silicon is the most abundant element in Earth's crust after oxygen. Slide 43 lists Tesla Silicon at 1.2 $/KWh, compared with 10.2 $/KWh for silicon structured in graphite.

**What cost reduction goal does the Tesla deck state for battery cells?**

Slide 15 is titled We Have A Plan To Halve The Cost Per KWh. It introduces the topics of cell design, cell factory, anode materials, cathode materials and cell vehicle integration.

Overlay: a black tint of roughly 40% opacity over the entire photo. The image is not cropped into a shape and is not full-bleed.

**Review notes**

**Layout**

Two-by-two grid of photographic tiles centred under a centred title, on a full black slide. Title sits at the top centre; the four tiles occupy the lower two-thirds with equal gutters of about 1.5% of slide width between columns and rows, left margin about 2.8% and right margin about 2.8%. Each caption is inset about 2.8% from the left edge of its tile and sits in the lower-left area of its tile, aligned left. No footer, no slide number, no logo outside the photos.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a 2x2 grid, each a rectangle with small rounded corners and full-bleed inside its frame. Top-left: a white Tesla Semi truck with grey trailer and a red Model 3, a white Model S and a dark grey Model X parked in a row on grey asphalt in front of a port with gantry cranes and containers, warm low sun and orange-tinged sky at the horizon, cropped at the top and sides, with a dark gradient darkening the left edge. Top-right: a red Model 3 in right-facing profile at speed on a road with horizontal motion blur in the background, grey-brown dusky sky, dark vignette on the left, lit from the right by warm low light. Bottom-left: a long row of white Tesla stationary battery cabinets on reddish-brown dirt with black pads, with green farmland, forested hills and a heavy grey cloud sky behind, diffuse overcast light, a Tesla wordmark visible on one cabinet, dark gradient on the left and bottom. Bottom-right: a two-storey suburban house with grey pitched roof and solar roof tiles, white garage door, warm lit windows, a grey Tesla Model S parked in the driveway with a charger on the wall beside the garage, green lawn and stepping stones, dusk light, dark gradient on the left and a subtle darkening at the bottom.

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed. Black full-bleed background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title and captions and no accent colours. Captions in Gotham SSm Bold, all capitals, wide letter-spacing, about 1.7% of slide height, white. ### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

To achieve the transition to sustainable energy,
we must produce more affordable EVs and
Energy Storage, while building factories faster
and with far less investment

**Layout**

Single centred text block with no title, footer or slide number. The four lines are centre-aligned and sit in the middle band of the slide, roughly between 40% and 61% of the height, with the block horizontally centred (about 19% to 82% of the width). The line pitch is about 5.6% of the slide height.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed monochrome texture behind the text: a dense field of fine white speckled, dithered vertical streaks that lean slightly diagonally, scattered across a black ground. The streaks are brightest and sharpest in thin lines and fade into grain toward the edges. There is no recognisable subject, no people, no logos, no charts and no overlay other than the black base.

**Design**

Background: Black #000000 with a full-bleed white speckled streak texture image on top. Black background (#000000) with a white speckled texture. Text is white (#FFFFFF). Typeface is Gotham SSm Medium, a geometric sans-serif with medium weight and normal case, with slightly open letter-spacing. Body text is about 4.3% of slide height per line; there is no separate title size. No lines, borders, shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients are visible.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A large bar chart that sets out the 100x growth needed in each vehicle segment makes the scale of the problem visible at a glance.
- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.
- Review: Small caption text on some diagrams (slides 6, 67) is only fit for a large screen, and a few pages carry little content beyond a photo and a stat, but this suits a live keynote with narration.

**Layout**

Single centred composition on a black slide: centred title near the top, centred uppercase subtitle beneath it, then one dark grey rounded panel spanning almost the full width (about 2.7% to 97.2% of the width, 28.3% to 77.9% of the height) holding two horizontal bar rows with their values at the right and five vehicle categories with images beneath. A centred small source line sits near the bottom. No slide number or logo is visible; margins are about 2.7% on each side.

**Visuals**

Bar chart drawn as two horizontal rows inside a dark grey panel. Row 1 is a single long grey bar labelled IN 2019 with the value 0.1 TWh at its right end. Row 2 is a segmented row of five red bars whose lengths are proportional to their GWh values: 1500 GWh (small/robotaxi), 3500 GWh (compact/midsize), 900 GWh (luxury and SUV), 1100 GWh (pickup truck), 3000 GWh (semi and commercial); the total is shown at the right as 10 TWh. Values are printed inside each red bar; no axes, scale ticks or legend are printed. Below the second row, five category labels are separated by thin vertical grey divider lines. Beneath the labels are five greyscale rendered vehicle images: a white covered shape (robotaxi, cropped by its box, soft shadow), a silver compact sedan (Model 3 style, three-quarter view facing right, soft shadow beneath), a silver crossover SUV (Model X style, side three-quarter view facing right) overlapping a grey angular pickup truck (cybertruck style, facing right, dark shadow), and a silver tractor-trailer cab (semi style, three-quarter view facing right, dark shadow). All images are cut out on the dark panel with no boxes or borders; lighting is a soft studio highlight; dominant colours are white, silver and black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Pure black full-bleed background. Title in white GothamSSm-Medium sans-serif, mixed case, wide letter-spacing, about 3.5% of slide height. Subtitle in white GothamSSm-Bold uppercase, wide tracking, about 1.9% of slide height. Panel fill #242424 with rounded corners of roughly 0.5% of width and no visible stroke. Bar row 1 fill #5E5E5E with a red accent edge #E53C24 on its left. Red bars #E53C24 with no corner radius and no shadow. Values inside the bars are white GothamSSm-Bold uppercase, about 1.7% of slide height. Total values 0.1 and 10 are white GothamSSm-Medium about 3.5% of slide height, with TWh labels in grey #929292 GothamSSm-Bold about 1.7%. Category labels are white GothamSSm-Bold uppercase about 1.5% of slide height with wide tracking and two-line centred layout. Divider lines thin, grey, about 0.1% of width. Source line white GothamSSm-Bold uppercase about 1.1%.

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Layout**

Centred composition on a black slide. The title sits centred near the top at about 10% of the slide height, with a centred uppercase subtitle beneath it at about 23%. A single dark grey rounded panel spans almost the full width (x 2.7% to 97.2%, y 28.3% to 77.9%) and holds two horizontal bar rows, a row of six category labels with thin vertical dividers, and six grey pictograms. The row of red bars is aligned to the left margin at x 4.7% and ends at x 94.5%, with bar totals at the right in the margin. The source line is centred at about 92% of the slide height. No page number is visible.

**Visuals**

Bar chart drawn as two rows of rectangles, with no axis or gridlines. Row 1 (top): one long grey bar titled 'IN 2019' with a thin red left edge, labelled at the right end as 0.006 TWh. Row 2: six red bars of unequal width, each with a value label inside: 2300 GWh (non renewable electricity), 1400 GWh (coal heating), 2100 GWh (gas heating), 800 GWh (oil heating), 1500 GWh (future EV), 1900 GWh (future growth). The bars are not drawn to a common scale; their widths do not follow the values. Row 2 total is labelled at the right as 10 TWh. Below the bars, six category labels sit in uppercase grey-white text, separated by five thin vertical grey divider lines. Under each label is a monochrome grey pictogram: an electricity utility pole with transformer (non renewable electricity); a shovel with a pile of coal lumps (coal heating); a pipe fitting with three flanged joints (gas heating); a steel oil drum (oil heating); a silver electric sedan in three-quarter view with a soft shadow (future EV); a white hard hat (future growth). All pictograms are greyscale photographic renders on the dark panel, with no framing or boxing. No legend and no highlighted points beyond the red bars.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background with a dark charcoal panel (#242424) and rounded corners of about 1% of slide width. Title and category labels are white (#FFFFFF). Secondary labels 'IN 2019' and 'TWh' are mid grey (#929292). The 2019 bar is #5E5E5E. Accent colour is red-orange #E53C24 for the six GWh bars and the thin left edge of the 2019 bar. Typeface is Gotham (geometric sans-serif). Title is Gotham Medium, about 3.5% of slide height, with generous letter-spacing. Subtitle is Gotham Bold, about 1.9% of slide height, all capitals and widely tracked. Category labels are Gotham Bold, about 1.5% of slide height, uppercase and widely tracked. Bar labels are Gotham Bold, about 1.7% of slide height. Source line is Gotham Bold, about 1.1% of slide height, uppercase. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients, except the soft shadow under the car pictogram.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

Goal One Problem: Today’s Battery Factories Cannot Scale Fast Enough / 0.15 = / 1 / GIGAFACTORY / TWh / $2T / INVESTMENT IN MATERIALS, CELL, / AND BATTERY MANUFACTURING / 2.8M / TOTAL PEOPLE ESTIMATED

**Layout**

Single title line centred across the top at about 10% of slide height, with no footer or slide number. Below it, two equal rounded panels sit side by side as two columns: left panel x 2.7 to 49.3%, right panel x 50.6 to 97.2%, both spanning y 28.3 to 95.2%, leaving about 2.7% side margins and about 4.8% bottom margin. The left panel is centred on a solar-cell image with a large equation, and the right panel stacks two centred figures separated by a thin horizontal rule at about y 62%.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style illustration of a solar cell in the left panel: a rectangular monocrystalline cell with chamfered (cut) top-left, top-right, bottom-left and bottom-right corners, shown head-on, centred in the panel and not cropped. Its surface is dark navy blue (about #2A2F45 to #1E2336) with a fine grid of silver-grey busbar and finger lines and four vertical thicker dividing lines. The image is slightly soft and blurred, has an even flat front light with no visible shadows, and its edge is a light grey (about #6E6E6E) bevel. It is boxed by the cell outline, with no overlay or tint. Right panel: one thin horizontal divider line, colour about #333333, about 0.2% of slide height thick, spanning x 52.5 to 95.4% at y about 62%. No other graphics, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 behind everything. Solid black background (#000000) with two flat dark grey panels (#242424) having small corner radii of about 0.4% of slide width and no shadow or gradient. Title text is white (#FFFFFF), set in Gotham SSm Medium, a geometric sans-serif, with wide letter-spacing and mixed case, about 3.5% of slide height. Large figures are white (#FFFFFF) in Gotham SSm Medium, about 8.2% of slide height (capital height about 5.7%). Captions are Gotham SSm Bold, all capitals, letter-spaced, about 1.7% of slide height, in mid grey #929292, with ESTIMATED in darker grey #5E5E5E. Lines are hairline and no accent colours are used.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

Goal One Problem: Today’s Battery Factories Cannot Scale Fast Enough / 20 / = / 135 / TWh / GIGAFACTORIES / $2T / INVESTMENT IN MATERIALS, CELL, / AND BATTERY MANUFACTURING / 2.8M / TOTAL PEOPLE / ESTIMATED

**Layout**

A single centred title sits across the top of the slide at about 10% from the top, above two equal dark panels side by side that fill the lower two-thirds with about 2.7% side margins and a gap of about 4% between them. The left panel holds a 9-column by 15-row grid of cell graphics with the figures 20 = 135 overlaid across its middle rows. The right panel is split by a thin horizontal rule at about 62% height, with $2T in the upper half and 2.8M in the lower half, both centred. There is no footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a grid of 135 identical solar-cell-style rectangles arranged in 9 columns and 15 rows inside the left panel, spanning about x 5.3% to 46.7% and y 32.9% to 90.3% of the slide. Each cell is a rounded rectangle about 3.9% wide by 2.7% high with a dark navy-grey fill (about #2A3142) showing a fine light grid of busbars, and a thin light-grey outline (about #8A8A8A, about 1 px). Cells are evenly spaced with horizontal gaps of about 0.9% and vertical gaps of about 1%, and no cell is cropped. The cell grid is flat, with no shadow, and the overlaid white numerals sit on top of cells in the middle rows. No charts, photographs or logos are present.

**Design**

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge the tiny labels, axis text and source lines on chart and diagram slides such as 6, 10, 24 and 67, and fix the chart on slide 10, which has no value labels.
- What would change the score: I would label the key values on the line charts (slides 10, 11, 68) and make the slide 67 waterfall bars carry more of the color emphasis so the totals read faster.

**Text**

Goal Two: More Affordable Cells / $/kWh / INDUSTRY TREND / FORECAST / 0 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025

**Layout**

Black slide with a centred title near the top, a line chart filling the middle, and a row of x-axis year labels along the bottom. The y-axis unit label $/kWh sits top-left above the plot, and two legend pills sit top-right, aligned to the same row. Horizontal gridlines span the full width from roughly x 2.8% to 97.2%; there is no footer and no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Line chart. Title: none separate from the slide title. Y-axis: $/kWh, labelled at top-left, with only the baseline value 0 printed; gridlines at equal intervals (about 11 gridlines above the baseline), no other values printed, so values are estimated: about 1.0 at 2013 peak descending. X-axis: years 2012 to 2025 printed at each tick, no unit. Series 1, INDUSTRY TREND (solid grey line, about #6B6B73, roughly 0.4% of slide height thick): starts at 2013 at a high value (about 9.5 $/kWh on the scale, the top of the curve near y 30%), falls steeply to about 2014 then flattens, passing about 2016 near the middle, reaching about 2020 at a low point and ending at 2021 (about 2.9 relative to the 0 baseline). Series 2, FORECAST (dashed white line, about #E0E0E0, dash length about 1.5% of width): continues from 2021 at the end of the solid line, declining gently to 2025 at about 2.6 on the same scale. No data labels, no highlighted points. Legend: two rounded pills, INDUSTRY TREND (grey pill) and FORECAST (light grey pill).

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background. Solid black background, minimal flat look. Title in white (#FFFFFF) geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like, medium weight), letter-spaced, about 3.5% of slide height. Axis labels in mid-grey #929292, sans-serif, about 1.6% of slide height; $/kWh and 0 in bold white. Gridlines thin dark grey (#2A2A2A), about 0.1% thick. Legend pills have fully rounded ends (radius equal to half height); INDUSTRY TREND pill fill about #6B6B73 with white bold caps text, FORECAST pill fill about #D4D4D4 with black bold caps text. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.
- What would change the score: I would label the key values on the line charts (slides 10, 11, 68) and make the slide 67 waterfall bars carry more of the color emphasis so the totals read faster.

**Text**

Goal Two Problem: EV Market Share Is Growing / But EVs Still Aren’t Accessible To All / EV MARKET SHARE / 2.0% / 1.8% / 1.6% / 1.4% / 1.2% / 1.0% / 0.8% / 0.6% / 0.4% / 0.2% / 0.0% / 2009 / 2010 / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / SOURCE: EV-VOLUMES.COM / OICA

**Layout**

A single full-slide chart on a black field. The two-line title is centred near the top, with the title block spanning roughly 10% to 18% of the height. A red pill label sits at the top right of the chart area, and the chart runs from about 31% to 86% of the height across roughly 3% to 97% of the width. Year labels sit in a row beneath the baseline, and a centred source line sits near the bottom at about 92%. There is no visible slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Chart: filled line chart with an area fill, titled by the red pill label 'EV MARKET SHARE'. Y axis is percent of market share, labelled 0.0% to 2.0% in 0.2 steps, with horizontal gridlines at each step. X axis is years, labelled 2009 to 2019 at one-year intervals. Series 'EV market share' is a single red line (about #E8392A, roughly 0.6 of the slide's line weight in the image) with a gradient area fill beneath it, from about #521410 near the line to black at the baseline. Printed values: none; the values below are estimates. About 0.02% in 2009, about 0.03% in 2010, about 0.06% in 2011, about 0.07% in 2012, about 0.11% in 2013, about 0.22% in 2014, about 0.27% in 2015, about 0.49% in 2016, about 0.90% in 2017, about 1.48% in 2018, about 1.85% in 2019. Legend: none; the red pill labelled 'EV MARKET SHARE' serves as the series label. Highlighted points: none; the line ends at the 2019 point, which is the last value and is not marked. The gridlines are thin grey (about #2A2A2A), and the area fill stops at the 2019 vertical.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000). Titles are white (#FFFFFF) in GothamSSm-Medium, a geometric sans-serif with normal weight and generous letter-spacing, with the title at about 3.5% of slide height per line. Body and axis labels are grey (#929292) GothamSSm-Bold, about 1.57% of slide height. The source line is white (#FFFFFF) GothamSSm-Bold in capitals, about 1.11% of slide height, with letter-spacing. ### Slide 12

**Text**

To Make The Best Cars In The World,
We Design Vehicles And Factories From The Ground Up

**Layout**

A single centred column on a black field: a two-line title sits near the top, centred horizontally, and a wide photograph band spans nearly the full slide width beneath it. The title is set in two centred lines with wide letter-spacing. Margins are about 2.8% on each side of the photo, with a large empty black area below it and no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a wide landscape band of an automotive body-welding cell. Subject: several orange KUKA industrial robot arms with grey and silver welding and clamping tooling, blue and green cable harnesses, and a partly assembled silver car body frame in the centre. Action: the robots are working on the body, with a shower of bright white weld sparks rising in the upper centre and right. Composition: a horizontal band, shown as a wide crop, with robots framing the left and right edges and the body in the middle. Lighting: bright, even factory light with cool highlights on the metal. Dominant colours: orange #E8561F, silver-grey #B8BCC2, black #101010, blue #1F4FA3. The photo is boxed, not full-bleed, with small rounded corners and no overlay or tint. A KUKA logo is visible on several robot arms, and a grey KUKA controller box is at lower left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title text. A white full-slide fill lies beneath a black full-slide fill, so only black shows. Title in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham SSm Medium style), medium weight, wide letter-spacing, title case, about 3.5% of slide height, white. There is no body text. The photo has small rounded corners of about 0.5% of slide height and no shadow, border or gradient.

### Slide 14

BATTERY DAY

**Layout**

No title placeholder, footer, page number or other text; margins are not applicable because the lockup floats on the textured background.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed monochrome photograph-like texture of diagonal streaks: many fine, grainy white and light-grey particle streaks running from lower left to upper right at a steep angle, fading into black, resembling a dithered or stippled flow field. Streaks are densest in the middle and lower half and sparse at the top edge. Composition is full-bleed with no border, no crop of the lockup, and no overlay other than the image itself. Lighting is uniform, no shading. Dominant colours are black #000000 with white #FFFFFF and light grey streaks. One logo: the Tesla T mark (white, about 4.5% of slide width, sitting at left of the lockup, roughly x 32.5 to 35.8, y 26.5 to 29.5) followed by the wordmark BATTERY DAY.

**Design**

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

We Have A Plan To Halve The Cost Per KWh / CELL DESIGN / CELL FACTORY / ANODE MATERIALS / CATHODE / MATERIALS / CELL VEHICLE / INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Five grey cards arranged in two centred rows: three cards across the top row and two centred below it, with the title centred above the grid at about 10% from the top. The slide has no footer, no page number and no logo, and the cards sit on a black field with wide side margins.

**Visuals**

Five greyscale product-style images, one inside each card, all centred horizontally in their cards and placed above the card label. Top row, left to right: a single upright silver cylindrical battery cell; a rectangular silver-grey cell factory unit holding two tiers of cylindrical cells on a grey base plate; a dark silver lumpy anode material chunk. Bottom row, left to right: a grey granular spherical cathode material ball with a rough, textured surface; a silver-grey vehicle body frame with a battery tray and grid structure beneath it, shown at an angle. Each image is a transparent cut-out on the grey card with no border or shadow. Lighting is soft studio lighting from above, and the palette is silver, grey and black.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Solid black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. The title is white (#FFFFFF), set in a wide-tracked geometric sans-serif, medium weight, with capitalised words. Card labels are black (#000000), bold, uppercase, wide letter-spacing, in a geometric sans-serif such as Gotham. Accent colour is light grey #D5D5D5 on the cards. Cards are flat rectangles with small rounded corners (about 0.5% of slide width) and no shadow, stroke or gradient. The title is about 3.5% of slide height and the card labels about 1.7% of slide height.

### Slide 16

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

CELL DESIGN
CELL FACTORY
ANODE MATERIALS
CATHODE MATERIALS
CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Two-part arrangement on a black field: a large open dashed ring occupies the left third, and a grid of five tiles sits on the right, three tiles in the top row and two centred beneath them. There is no title, footer, logo or slide number. Tile labels are centred under each image, with generous margins on all sides.

**Visuals**

Five small photographic images, one per tile, each on a dark background: (1) a silver metallic cylindrical battery cell, standing upright, photographed in front view with soft studio lighting and a bright highlight on its shiny body, shown in greyscale; (2) a cell-factory assembly machine, a grey, boxy multi-part industrial unit with cylindrical stacks and connectors on top, in greyscale and dimly lit; (3) an irregular, crumpled dark-grey anode-material lump, greyscale, dim lighting; (4) a dark-grey, matte, granular sphere representing cathode material, greyscale, dim lighting; (5) a grey wireframe-like car body frame, a vehicle chassis and roll-cage structure with a floor-pan element, greyscale, dim lighting. All photographs are cut out with no visible boxes and sit within their tiles. A decorative open ring made of about eight separate dark-grey rounded arc segments with gaps forms a circle on the left, with no text or fill inside it.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. Tile labels are set in uppercase geometric sans-serif bold (GothamSSm-Bold style) with wide letter-spacing, sized about 1.67% of slide height. The active tile label is black #000000 and the inactive tile labels are set to #FFFFFF in the file, though they appear as a muted mid-grey in the rendered image. Tile fills are #D5D5D5 for the active tile and #242424 for the others, with small corner radii of about 0.5% of slide width and no shadows. The ring is #333333-ish dark grey with round line caps, roughly 1.5% of slide width in stroke weight. There are no titles or body paragraphs.

CAP
CAN

**Layout**

Single centred object on a full-bleed black field with two callout labels; no title, footer or slide number. The cylinder sits slightly left of centre, vertically centred in the lower two-thirds, with the CAP label at upper right and the CAN label at left-middle, each joined to the cell by a thin horizontal leader line.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a single upright cylindrical lithium-ion style battery cell (18650 format) standing on a dark textured surface, viewed straight on at eye level, slightly above the base. The cell is a dark grey metal can with a rolled, ringed top cap, a small raised central vent/terminal visible on top, and a soft shadow falling to the right of its base. Lighting is soft and low-key from the front-left, producing gentle highlights on the cap rim and a subtle sheen on the can. Dominant colours are near-black background and charcoal grey metal. The image is full-bleed, not cropped, with no overlay or tint. Two callouts: a white thin line (about 0.2% of slide height) runs horizontally from the top rim of the cap at about x 55%, y 24% to a label 'CAP' at about x 71%, y 25%; a second white thin line runs horizontally from the left edge of the can at about x 50%, y 46% leftward to a label 'CAN' at about x 26%, y 46%. Both labels are white uppercase geometric sans-serif (Futura/Century Gothic-like), medium weight, letter-spaced slightly.

**Design**

Background: Near-black #0A0A0A photographic backdrop with a dark textured surface (full-bleed photograph).

+ / − / CATHODE / SEPARATOR / ANODE

**Layout**

No title and no footer; the slide is a full-bleed photograph with labels overlaid. A horizontal battery-cell cutaway sits centred in the lower-middle band, with a small gold battery icon at left and labels on the right and left, each joined to its part by a thin white leader line. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of a cylindrical battery cell cut away to show its layers, lying on dark charcoal fabric. The cell is seen from the side at eye level, slightly low, centred horizontally, filling about 38% of the slide width. The cylinder's outer casing is pale silver-grey with a white highlight along the top. A cutaway band shows the layers: a mid-grey cathode band, a white rolled separator strip beneath it, and a tan-gold anode sheet at the bottom. A flat silver tab with a gold tip extends left from the cell (positive end, marked +), and a flat silver tab extends right (negative end, marked −). Lighting is soft studio light from above and front, leaving the background in deep shadow. Dominant colours: black, charcoal, silver-grey, gold. The image is not cropped and has no overlay or tint other than the drawn gold icon and labels. A flat gold battery-level icon of eight stacked cells, with a white terminal cap on top and white cell dividers, sits to the left of the cell at about 12% x and 46% y. Gold plus and minus symbols mark the two terminals. Three white thin leader lines run from the labels to the layers: CATHODE line to the cathode band, SEPARATOR line to the separator strip, ANODE line to the anode sheet.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph of dark charcoal-black fabric (#0E0E0E to #2A2A2A). Black photographic background with no gradient. Labels are white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric sans-serif, similar to Montserrat, regular weight, all capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 3.0% of slide height. Accents are gold (#E8A917) for the battery icon and the + and − symbols. Leader lines are thin white strokes about 1 px wide. There are no shapes, corner radii or shadows apart from the photograph's own soft shading.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Review: Each page carries one idea, with one accent color, one typeface and consistent titles, and the visuals do the explaining: the cell animation on slides 17 to 22, the wet versus dry electrode comparison on slides 32 and 34, and the blocks on slides 50 and 51, where the empty space in the dry-process column makes the point.

**Text**

DISCHARGE
+
Li+ (about 28 labels)
CATHODE
SEPARATOR
ANODE
−

**Layout**

No title or footer; the slide is a full-bleed photographic render with the battery cell centred horizontally, slightly below mid-height. A discharge battery-level icon sits at the left and the CATHODE, SEPARATOR and ANODE callouts sit at the right and left edges, each with a thin leader line to its part. No slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style 3D render of a cylindrical lithium battery cell in cutaway, lying horizontally on a dark charcoal fabric surface, full-bleed. The cell has a light grey metal can at the top, a grey cathode band across the middle filled with about 28 yellow 'Li+' ion discs, a pale grey separator sheet below it, and a tan/gold anode strip along the base. A silver positive tab with a gold section projects from the left end, marked '+' in gold; a silver negative tab projects from the right end, marked '−' in gold. A thin white circuit line runs from the positive tab up, across the top and down to the negative tab, with a row of about seven small glowing yellow dots along the top segment and about four along the right descending segment, indicating electron flow. Left of the cell is a vertical battery-level icon labelled DISCHARGE: a white outlined rectangle with a small cap on top, divided into seven segments, the bottom five segments filled yellow and the top two black. Lighting is a soft spotlight from above and front, with dark vignetting; dominant colours are black, charcoal grey, silver, gold and yellow. The image is full-bleed, not cropped or boxed, with no tint overlay beyond the darkening of the background.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed dark charcoal-black fabric photograph, approximately #111111 to #2A2A2A. Black background with a charcoal fabric texture. Labels are white sans-serif capitals (geometric sans such as Montserrat, regular weight, letter-spaced) at about 2.5% of slide height for DISCHARGE and about 2.6% for CATHODE, SEPARATOR and ANODE. ### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Review: Each page carries one idea, with one accent color, one typeface and consistent titles, and the visuals do the explaining: the cell animation on slides 17 to 22, the wet versus dry electrode comparison on slides 32 and 34, and the blocks on slides 50 and 51, where the empty space in the dry-process column makes the point.
- Worth keeping: Keep the progress ring and tile tracker, the single red accent against black, the animated cell build on slides 17 to 22, and the sparse before and after process diagrams on slides 50 and 51.

**Text**

CHARGING / ANODE / CATHODE / SEPARATOR / Li+ (repeated about 31 times as ion markers on the anode and separator)

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark photographic scene with a single centred subject. A cylindrical cell lies horizontally in the centre-right, with a battery-level icon and the CHARGING label at the left, and white leader-line callouts for ANODE, CATHODE and SEPARATOR. No title bar, footer, page number or margins are printed; all labels sit on the image.

**Visuals**

Illustration of a cutaway cylindrical lithium-ion cell lying on its side on a dark textured surface. The cell body is a silver-grey cylinder with a lighter grey cathode band and a pale grey separator sheet at the lower edge. At the bottom, a bronze-gold anode sheet is covered with about 31 yellow glowing circular Li+ ion markers, each with the text Li+ inside. A thin white outline wire loops from the positive tab on the left (a silver tab with a gold end, marked by a gold plus sign) up to the top, across to the right and down to the negative silver tab on the right (marked by a gold minus sign). About 20 small glowing yellow dots run along this wire to show electron flow, and the dots are denser at the bends. On the left, a battery icon with a white outline has a small white terminal cap on top and six horizontal cells: the top three are black and the bottom five are filled gold. Three thin white leader lines point to CATHODE (to the grey band), SEPARATOR (to the pale sheet) and ANODE (to the bronze sheet). Composition is a horizontal eye-level shot, centred, with the cell occupying about the middle third of the frame. Lighting is soft and studio-like from above, with the black fabric background falling into deep shadow. Dominant colours are black, charcoal grey, silver, gold and bronze. The photograph is full-bleed with no crop, border or frame, and no tint overlay other than the darkening vignette.

**Design**

Background: Near-black charcoal #0E0E10 photographic background of dark fabric with a faint grey sand-like texture in the lower half. Dark, cinematic technical-illustration look. The background is near-black charcoal fabric. Labels are white uppercase in a geometric sans-serif such as Montserrat, medium weight, with slight letter-spacing and cap height about 2.2% of slide height. Accent colour is gold #F5B800 used for the Li+ markers, electron dots, battery fill and polarity signs. Body and label text is white #FFFFFF. Leader lines are thin white strokes about 0.1% of slide height. The battery icon has a 2 px white outline. No corner radii, gradients on shapes or drop shadows other than a soft glow around the gold dots and Li+ markers.

Two photographic renderings of cylindrical metal battery cells, standing upright, same scale, placed side by side. Right cell: labelled 2170 above and 2017 below, a larger silver-grey cylinder with a rounded top cap, lit from the upper left with soft gradient shading to dark grey on the right edge. Between them a thin light-grey (#929292) horizontal line with an open arrowhead pointing right, running from just right of the left cell to just left of the right cell at about mid-height of the cells. Above the arrow, centred: '+50%' with 'ENERGY' beneath it. Below the arrow, centred: 'EVOLUTION'. No chart, table or logo.

Background: Solid black #000000. Plain black background (#000000) with no texture. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif, Gotham (GothamSSm) in Medium for the numerals and '+50%' and Bold for the uppercase labels with open letter-spacing. The '+50%' is about 3.5% of slide height; the labels and years about 1.7% to 2.0% of slide height. Line is a thin grey stroke (#929292) about 0.3% of slide height with a small open arrowhead. The cells are photographs with no borders, shadows or rounded containers.

**Layout**

Single full-width chart on a black slide. The title is centred horizontally near the top (about 10% from the top). The y-axis label IMPROVEMENT sits top left at the start of the axis column, and three legend pills sit top right on the same row as the NEW CELL marker. The plot runs from about 3% to 97% of the width, with the x-axis tick labels beneath it and the centred x-axis title CELL OUTER DIAMETER MM near the bottom (about 91%). There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Line chart with two series on a black background. Title: none inside the chart; the slide title Bigger Cylindrical Cells Cost Less serves as the heading. Y axis: IMPROVEMENT, 0% to 20%, gridlines and labels at 0%, 5%, 10%, 15% and 20%. X axis: CELL OUTER DIAMETER (MM), 21 to 61 mm, labelled every 5 mm (21, 26, 31, 36, 41, 46, 51, 56, 61). Series 1, COST REDUCTION, red line: about 0% at 21 mm, about 5.5% at 24 mm, about 9% at 27 mm, about 11.7% at 30 mm, about 13.6% at 33 mm, about 15.2% at 36 mm, about 16.5% at 39 mm, about 18.1% at 46 mm, about 19.0% at 51 mm, about 19.5% at 56 mm, about 19.4% at 61 mm; values are estimates because no values are printed. Series 2, VEHICLE RANGE, orange line: about 0% at 21 mm, about 1.4% at 24 mm, about 2.6% at 27 mm, about 3.7% at 30 mm, about 4.7% at 33 mm, about 5.5% at 36 mm, about 6.0% at 39 mm, about 6.7% at 46 mm, about 6.8% at 49 mm, about 6.7% at 51 mm, about 6.0% at 56 mm, about 5.7% at 61 mm; values are estimates. Both lines are smooth-ish polylines of about 4 px stroke width, the red line in #E63B24 and the orange line in #E8A04C. Legend: three pill-shaped labels, NEW CELL in grey, VEHICLE RANGE in orange, COST REDUCTION in red, each with white letter-spaced caps text. Highlighted point: a vertical grey reference line at 46 mm, the NEW CELL marker, running from the pill down to the 0% baseline, with the red and orange lines crossing it at about 18% and about 6.7%. Gridlines: thin dark grey horizontal lines at each 5% step.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background #000000 with no gradient or texture. Title in white #FFFFFF, geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style), medium weight, letter-spaced slightly, about 3.5% of slide height. Axis tick labels and CELL OUTER DIAMETER unit in mid grey #929292, bold sans-serif, about 1.6% to 1.9% of slide height. Axis title CELL OUTER DIAMETER and IMPROVEMENT in white #FFFFFF, bold, all caps, letter-spaced, about 1.3% of slide height. Accents: red #E63B24, orange #E8A04C, grey #888888. Gridlines dark grey #2A2A2A, about 0.2% of slide height. Pills have fully rounded ends (radius about half their height), no shadow, no border. Series lines have round caps.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred title near the top, a single line chart filling the middle and lower area, and a small label row at upper left. The title is centred horizontally at about 10 percent from the top. The y-axis label sits at the upper left at about 24.5 percent from the top, aligned to a left margin of about 3 percent. The x-axis tick labels run along a baseline at about 86 percent from the top, and the centred axis title CELL OUTER DIAMETER MM sits at about 91 percent. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Line chart with a single curved series and no legend. Title: The Challenge With Big Cells Is Supercharging. Y-axis: titled SUPERCHARGE TIME INCREASE, no units and no numeric tick labels; only a zero label is printed at the baseline; five horizontal gridlines are evenly spaced above the baseline with no values printed (values about 0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100 of the unlabelled scale are not printed, so none are stated). X-axis: CELL OUTER DIAMETER in MM, labelled ticks 21, 26, 31, 36, 41, 46, 51, 56, 61, with the axis running from about 21 to about 61. Series CHARGE TIME (1 TAB): a red curve starting at about x=21 at the baseline (about 0), rising slowly to about 26 then increasingly steeply; about 0.46 of the gridline span at x=46 (estimated, no value printed); ending at about x=57.5 at about 0.92 of the top gridline span (estimated, no value printed). The curve is a smooth convex (accelerating) line about 4 px thick. Highlighted point: a vertical grey reference line at x=46 marked by a grey pill label NEW CELL at the top, crossing the curve at about 0.46 of the span. The curve end is labelled CHARGE TIME (1 TAB) in red text to its right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Minimalist dark chart style. Background pure black #000000. Title in white #FFFFFF, Gotham-style geometric sans-serif, medium weight, widely letter-spaced, about 3.5 percent of slide height. Axis labels and tick labels: bold geometric sans (Gotham-like), uppercase for the labels, letter-spaced, white #FFFFFF for the y-axis label and x-axis title, grey #929292 for tick numbers and MM. Series label CHARGE TIME (1 TAB) in red #E8412C, regular weight, uppercase, about 1.3 percent of slide height. Curve red #E8412C, about 4 px stroke, rounded ends. Gridlines are thin dark grey #2A2A2A hairlines spanning the plot width. Reference line is a solid grey #8C8C8C vertical stroke about 3 px wide. The NEW CELL pill is filled grey #8C8C8C with white bold uppercase text and fully rounded ends (pill shape). No shadows or gradients.

Single full-slide line chart on a black field with a centred title across the top third-to-upper area; the y-axis label sits top-left, the x-axis runs along the bottom with its label centred below the tick numbers, and the chart plots from left margin to right margin with horizontal gridlines spanning the width. **Visuals**

Line chart. Title: none separate beyond the slide headline. X-axis: CELL OUTER DIAMETER (MM), ticks labelled 21, 26, 31, 36, 41, 46, 51, 56, 61 mm, plotted from about 21 to about 59 mm. Y-axis: SUPERCHARGE TIME INCREASE, unitless, labelled only with 0 at the baseline; no other values printed; gridlines at five evenly spaced heights above baseline. Series 1: CHARGE TIME (1 TAB), dotted dark-red line rising from near zero at about 22 mm, curving upward steeply to about 80 percent of the top gridline height at about 58 mm; value at 46 mm about 35 percent of the top gridline height (estimate, no value printed). Series 2: CHARGE TIME (TABLESS), solid bright red line, thick, nearly flat at the baseline from 21 to about 40 mm, rising gently to about 4 percent of plot height at about 58 mm (estimate, no value printed). Legend: none as a box; series identified by red end labels at the line ends. Highlighted point: vertical grey line at 46 mm from baseline to the NEW CELL pill, which overlaps the red tabless line at its crossing.

Axis and series labels in white #FFFFFF GothamSSm Bold, uppercase, letter-spaced, about 1.3 percent of slide height. Series red labels in red #E8332A-like tone. NEW CELL pill fill grey #888888 with white bold uppercase text, fully rounded ends; vertical rule grey #8A8A8A about 2 px wide.

### Slide 27

**Text**

SIMPLER MANUFACTURING
FEWER PARTS
5X REDUCTION IN ELECTRICAL PATH

**Layout**

A single-column left-aligned text stack sits at the left edge, vertically centred on the slide, with a large circular photograph placed to its right, centred horizontally at about the middle of the slide. There is no title, footer or slide number, and the three text lines are spaced evenly at roughly 6.7% of slide height apart.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a copper coil or winding viewed straight on from one end, forming a circle. Its outer ring is a band of wide, curved copper segments separated by thin dark red-brown gaps, and the band tapers inward. Inside it, many thousands of thin copper bars lie in concentric rings that spiral toward a small central bore. The bore is a dark round opening with a black interior and a thin silver-grey rim with a faint highlight on its lower-left edge. The subject is centred with a small margin on all sides, shown full frame and not cropped, with no overlay or tint. Lighting is a warm front light with specular highlights on the copper, and the dominant colours are copper about #B87355, dark copper about #5A3428 and black #000000. The photograph is unboxed and sits directly on the black background.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Solid black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. All text is white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric sans-serif bold typeface, closest to GothamSSm Bold, set in all capitals with wide letter-spacing. Each line is about 1.85% of slide height in size, and capital height is about 1.3% of slide height. The only accent colour is the copper of the photograph. There are no shapes, rules or shadows.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Full-slide black background rectangle (Solid fill #000000, no border, no shadow. A white fill of the same size lies beneath it and is fully covered.)
- photo at x 5.4, y 5.2, w 89.6, h 89.6 [#B87355]: Photograph of a copper coil viewed axially: concentric rings of copper bars spiralling toward a central dark bore, with an outer band of wide copper segments (Placed image 1720 by 968 pixels, unboxed and not cropped, with no border, no shadow and no overlay. The visible copper disc is centred and sits on the black background. Dominant colours are copper #B87355 and black #000000.)

No title or footer. A single upright battery cell sits in the centre-left of the slide, with a vertical dimension line on its left and a horizontal dimension bracket beneath it. Three stacked statistics are centred in a right-hand column at about x 80%, evenly spaced from y 22% to y 86%. Margins are generous on all sides, and there is no slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a single cylindrical lithium-ion battery cell, standing upright and viewed slightly from above, with a crimped silver lid and a small raised vent at the top. The cell is brushed stainless or aluminium silver with vertical specular highlights, lit from the upper left, and it fades to black at the base. Its black surround blends into the slide background, so the photo reads as unboxed. Dimension annotations are thin grey (#929292) lines: a vertical line at about x 35.6% from y 11.4% to 79.7% with short end ticks, labelled '80 MM' rotated 90 degrees to read bottom-to-top; and a horizontal bracket at about y 88.8% from x 40.8% to 59.2% with upward end ticks, labelled '46 MM' beneath. Statistics are typeset numerals with no chart, icon, or logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Solid black background (#000000) throughout. Statistic numerals are white (#FFFFFF) in GothamSSm-Medium, a geometric sans-serif, at about 8.15% of slide height. Captions ENERGY, RANGE and POWER are in GothamSSm-Bold, all caps with wide letter-spacing, at about 1.67% of slide height, in grey #929292. Dimension value '80' and '46' are white bold, 'MM' is grey bold, both about 1.85% of slide height. Dimension lines are hairline grey (#929292). No shapes, shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

14%
$/KWH REDUCTION
14%
CELL FACTORY
ANODE MATERIALS
CATHODE
MATERIALS
CELL VEHICLE
INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Two-part horizontal split on a black field. A large circular progress ring with the figure 14% sits in the left third, centred at about x 24%, y 50%. On the right, five equal dark rectangular cards form a staggered grid: three in the top row and two centred in the row below, with a 1.5% gap between cards. No title, footer, slide number or logo is visible. The left and right groups are vertically centred on the slide.

**Visuals**

Chart: a circular progress ring (gauge). Type: donut/arc gauge. Title: none printed. Value: 14% printed in the centre, with the unit label '$/KWH REDUCTION' below it. The ring is a full circle drawn as a dark grey track (about #333333, roughly 1.3% of slide width stroke, round caps) with a white arc (#FFFFFF, same stroke width, round caps) overlaying the top segment from about 10 o'clock to about 2 o'clock. The track has a gap at the bottom between about 5 and 6 o'clock. No axes, legend or scale values are shown. Visual group: five cards, each a rounded rectangle with a small photograph and a label. Card 1 (dark #242424): a photographed cylindrical battery cell, dark grey metal, upright, centred, with the label '14%' in white bold below. Card 2 (light grey #D5D5D5, the only highlighted card): a photograph of a cell factory, a grey-silver stack of cylindrical cells with metal terminals on a square base, shown in full colour-neutral greyscale, with label 'CELL FACTORY' in black bold below. Card 3 (dark #242424): a greyscale photograph of an irregular dark grey anode material lump, label 'ANODE MATERIALS' below. Card 4 (dark #242424): a greyscale sphere with a grainy textured surface, label 'CATHODE / MATERIALS' below. Card 5 (dark #242424): a greyscale line drawing style photograph of a vehicle body frame cell-integration structure, label 'CELL VEHICLE / INTEGRATION' below. All photographs are isolated on their cards with no crop, lit from above with soft even lighting, neutral grey tones, no overlay. The inactive cards' images and labels appear dimmed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the entire slide. Black background (#000000) throughout with no gradient or texture. Titles and labels set in Gotham-style geometric sans-serif (GothamSSm), bold weight for labels, medium weight for the large figure. The large 14% is about 9.4% of slide height; small card labels are about 1.7% of slide height in all caps with wide letter-spacing. Colours: white #FFFFFF for the big figure, ring arc and inactive labels (dimmed in render); black #000000 for the highlighted label; accent is the light grey #D5D5D5 highlight card. Cards #242424 with small corner radius (about 0.5% of slide height). No shadows.

### Slide 30

**Text**

Inspiration / PRINTING / BOTTLING

**Layout**

Two equal-width image columns below a centred title on a black field. The title 'Inspiration' sits centred near the top at about 10% from the top edge. Each column has a centred uppercase label above its photograph, at about 23% from the top. The left photo spans roughly x 2.7% to 97.3% and the right photo sits in the right column with a gutter of about 2.7% between them; both images share the same top and bottom edges (about 28.2% to 77.8% of slide height) and are placed with equal margins of about 2.7% left and right. The lower band below the photos is empty black.

**Visuals**

Two photographs side by side, both boxed with small rounded corners.
Left photograph: PRINTING. Subject: a curved, looping overhead conveyor or track inside a warehouse-like industrial hall, carrying a long chain of white-and-grey paper or print-sheet products draped over a series of parallel steel rods that arc around a bend. Behind it is a corrugated metal roof with exposed white steel beams and diagonal bracing, with daylight visible at the lower left. Composition: wide landscape frame, camera looking up at the structure from below, the loop running diagonally from upper centre to lower left. Lighting: natural daylight from openings in the roof, with low-contrast grey shadows. Dominant colours: grey, off-white, rust brown, steel grey. Not cropped.
Right photograph: BOTTLING. Subject: a close, side-on view of a bottling line machine with a row of dark glass bottles standing in a line on a rotating star-wheel, a green guide rail curving beneath them, and stainless-steel filling and capping heads above. Composition: landscape frame, horizontal bands of machinery, bottles in the mid-lower third, machinery filling the top. Lighting: even indoor light with soft highlights on the steel. Dominant colours: charcoal and black, steel grey, bottle-green rails. Not cropped apart from the frame edges.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) across the whole slide. Title and labels in white (#FFFFFF). Title is a geometric sans-serif in a medium weight (Gotham-style), with normal case, about 3.5% of slide height for its size. Column labels are a bold geometric sans-serif (Gotham Bold style), all caps, white, about 1.9% of slide height, with wide letter-spacing. No accent colours in the typography; colour comes only from the photographs. Photographs have small rounded corners of roughly 0.3% of slide height and no shadow or border.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Simple is Hard / BENCH / LAB / PILOT

**Layout**

Three equal-width photographs sit side by side in columns across the middle band of the slide, each captioned by a centred uppercase label above it; the title is centred at the top. Margins are about 2.8% on the left and right, with the photo row ending near 78% of slide height and no footer or slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Three photographs of industrial roll-to-roll equipment, each boxed with slightly rounded corners and no overlay. Left photo (BENCH): a close view of a polished steel roller in the foreground with a dark spoon-like tool dipping into a dark viscous material on the roller surface; behind it a person in a red top and blue nitrile gloves, with a blue and white safety sign at the left edge. Middle photo (LAB): a grey-silver machine frame with a black carriage and small wheeled mechanism bridging a steel roller; a dark grey sheet or film is fed diagonally across the roller toward the lower left; dark background. Right photo (PILOT): a long receding row of large polished silver rollers in a multi-stand production line, with white and grey machine housings and bolted end plates on each side, bright white room behind; the foreground roller shows a thin band of coloured wires or strips. Lighting is soft and even in the pilot shot, warm highlights on the steel in the bench shot, and dim mixed light in the lab shot. Dominant colours: silver, grey, black, with red in the bench photo and white in the pilot photo. None of the photographs is cropped beyond rectangular framing.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-bleed background.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A two-column comparison of the 2018 Gigafactory and the Terafactory, with large figures for investment and footprint, carries the point with minimal text.

**Text**

-75% / INVESTMENT PER GWH / 10x / SMALLER FOOTPRINT PER GWH / 150 GWh / 1 TWh / 2018 GIGAFACTORY / TERAFACTORY / SIMPLER ACCELERATES TWH SCALE

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with two statistic blocks across the top, centred over the left and right halves. A wide dark-grey rounded panel spans the middle band, holding two solar-cell-style images side by side, each with a caption beneath. A single centred tagline sits near the bottom. There is no visible title bar, page number or footer, and no logo.

**Visuals**

Two chamfered-rectangle images, each a photograph-style illustration of a solar cell or module. Left: the 2018 Gigafactory cell, a full wide octagon-cornered panel of dark-blue polycrystalline cells (about #2A3F7A) with fine light-grey grid lines and thicker silver-grey (#D0D0D0) busbars forming four vertical divisions, lit evenly from the front, shown whole and not cropped, with the label '150 GWh' printed over its centre in white. Right: the Terafactory cell, the same octagon-cornered form drawn smaller, sitting on a lighter dark-grey (#3A3A3A) chamfered halo that is wider than the cell itself, with the same dark-blue cells and silver busbars divided into four sections, and the label '1 TWh' printed over its centre in white. The two statistics above are typographic only, with no chart, icon or logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the entire slide. Pure black (#000000) background with no gradient or texture. Titles and statistics are white (#FFFFFF) in GothamSSm Medium, a geometric sans-serif, at about 8.2% of slide height for the figures '-75%' and '10x'. Labels are small caps in GothamSSm Bold, letter-spaced, grey #929292 for the statistic captions and white #FFFFFF for the captions and tagline, at about 1.7% to 1.9% of slide height. Inside the panel, the cell labels are white GothamSSm Medium at about 3.3% of slide height. The main panel is a flat #242424 rectangle with a slightly rounded corner (about 0.5% of slide width) and no shadow or border. Images are unshadowed and unrotated.

### Slide 40

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

32%
$/KWH REDUCTION
14%
18%
ANODE MATERIALS
CATHODE
MATERIALS
CELL VEHICLE
INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a pure black field: a large partial ring with the headline figure sits in the left half, centred around x 24%, y 50%, and a staggered grid of five tiles fills the right half, with three tiles in the top row and two centred beneath them. There is no title, footer, slide number or margin text; the content floats in the middle of the slide with generous empty space above and below.

**Visuals**

Donut-style progress ring, about 32.7% of slide width and 58% of slide height, centred at roughly x 24%, y 50%. The ring is drawn as a thick round-capped arc (stroke about 1.2% of slide height) in two parts: a white arc (#FFFFFF) covering roughly the top and right side, from about 10 o'clock to about 4 o'clock, and a dark grey track (#333333) covering the remaining left and bottom portion, with a gap at the bottom centre. The white arc marks 32% of the ring. Centre of ring: the large figure '32%' and the caption '$/KWH REDUCTION'. Five tiles, each a rounded-corner rectangle with a photograph and a label: (1) battery cell, a grey cylindrical lithium-ion cell in a vertical orientation, dark grey to black metallic shading, label '14%'; (2) battery module, a dark grey mechanical assembly with cell-holder frame and terminals, label '18%'; (3) anode materials, a lumpy, shiny black-and-silver graphite-like rock fragment with light highlights, label 'ANODE MATERIALS'; (4) cathode materials, a dark grey granular spherical powder ball with a textured surface, label 'CATHODE MATERIALS'; (5) cell vehicle integration, a grey wireframe-like vehicle body structure with a floor pack, label 'CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION'. Photographs are monochrome, lit from above with soft highlights, on flat backgrounds, not cropped, not full-bleed; each sits inside its tile. Tile 3 is the only highlighted tile.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide.

**Text**

Silicon Is Awesome And Inexpensive
MOST ABUNDANT ELEMENT IN EARTH’S CRUST AFTER OXYGEN

**Layout**

Single full-width band with a centred title at the top and two left-aligned captions in a two-column row above a wide photograph box that spans nearly the full slide width. The title sits near the top at about 10% from the top edge, the captions at about 23%, and the photograph occupies roughly 28% to 78% of the height. The left caption starts at about 6% from the left edge and the right caption at about 60%, aligned to the photo's left and right thirds. No footer, no slide number and no logo are visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full colour not present; it is a greyscale close-up of a heap of metallic silicon lumps filling a rectangular box spanning x 2.8% to 97.2% and y 28.3% to 77.9% of the slide, pixel size 1813 by 536. Subject: angular, rough-surfaced silver-grey lumps of silicon piled loosely and seen from above and slightly in front, with bright specular highlights on their upper faces and dark shadowed gaps between them. Composition: edge-to-edge tiling filling the box, cropped by the box edges on all sides, no focal point, no people or text. Lighting: even, diffuse studio light with bright highlights and deep crevice shadows. Dominant colours: silver-grey #8C9096 with highlights near #D8DCE0 and shadows near #1E2024. The box has slightly rounded corners of about 0.3% of slide width and no border or shadow. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the full slide. Pure black background (#000000) with all text in white (#FFFFFF); there are no accent colours. The title is set in a geometric sans-serif, Gotham Medium, in sentence case, with generous letter-spacing, at about 3.5% of slide height. The two captions are Gotham Bold in all capitals with wide tracking, at about 1.85% of slide height, white. Body text is white and the same family. The photograph is a box with rounded corners of about 0.3% of slide width. There are no lines, shapes, gradients or shadows.

### Slide 42

**Text**

Challenge: Volume Expansion / 4X VOLUME EXPANSION / MANY CYCLES / MANY CYCLES / ELECTRICAL / ISOLATION / SEI LAYER / -Li+ / BROKEN / SEI LAYER / MANY CYCLES / THICK / SEI LAYER

**Layout**

Three equal dark-grey panels sit side by side across the lower two-thirds of the slide, with equal gutters of about 2 percent of slide width and a left margin of 2.8 percent and right margin of about 2.8 percent. The title is centred horizontally near the top, roughly 10 percent down from the top edge, with empty black space between the title and the panels. No footer, logo or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Three three-step particle diagrams, one per panel, drawn in soft grey-blue 3D render style. Left panel: a smooth sphere at the top, a downward arrow labelled 4X VOLUME EXPANSION to its right, then a downward arrow labelled MANY CYCLES on the left, leading to a sphere whose shell is cracked into shards at the middle, then a downward arrow leading to a fragmented, shard-covered sphere at the bottom. Middle panel: a flat grey-blue plate densely covered in small spheres at the top, a downward arrow labelled MANY CYCLES, then a plate where many spheres have scattered and separated into loose clusters at the bottom, with a thin-outlined circle around a few isolated spheres and a leader line from the circle down-right to the label ELECTRICAL ISOLATION. Right panel: a cluster of five to six spheres each with a thin grey shell at the top, a downward arrow labelled -Li+, a cluster of spheres with dashed, broken outer shells in the middle, a downward arrow labelled MANY CYCLES, and a cluster of spheres sitting inside a thick smooth dark-grey blob at the bottom, labelled THICK SEI LAYER. Illustrations are full-colour renders with soft studio lighting from upper left, shown unboxed against the panel fill, with no cropping. Dominant colours are light blue-grey #C5CAD6 for the spheres and mid grey #6B6B6B for the shells and plate.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

### Slide 43

**Text**

Current Approaches To Silicon Are Highly
Engineered Expensive Materials
SILICON STRUCTURED
IN SIO GLASS
6.6 $/KWh
SILICON STRUCTURED
IN GRAPHITE
10.2 $/KWh
SILICON
NANOWIRES
>100 $/KWh
TESLA
SILICON
1.2 $/KWh

**Layout**

Single centred title sits in the top band, two lines, centred on the slide at about 10% from the top. Below it, two dark rounded panels sit side by side on one row: a large panel spanning about 2.8% to 73.4% of the width holding three image-and-label columns, and a narrower panel from about 74.6% to 97.3% holding one column. Each column is a square image over a centred two-line caps label and a centred price. Left and right margins are about 2.8%, there is no footer, and there is no slide number.

**Visuals**

Four square grayscale microscopy and electron-microscope images, each boxed with small rounded corners and no overlay, arranged left to right in the panels. (1) Silicon structured in SiO glass: a transmission electron micrograph with a dark curved carbon shell running from top-left down to bottom-right, a dotted white line tracing the shell edge, grey granular matrix, a label 'SiOx matrix' at top centre, dashed white ellipses around crystalline lattice patches, white arrows pointing to a label 'Si', and a label 'Carbon shell' with an arrow pointing to the shell edge at lower left. (2) Silicon structured in graphite: a dark porous network of particles on a light grey ground, with a lighter inset rectangle in the upper right showing a grey particle, and a small red dashed circle near the bottom edge around one particle. (3) Silicon nanowires: a scanning electron micrograph of dense upright grey wire-like columns seen from the side, filling the square with light and shadow variation. (4) Tesla silicon: a grey electron micrograph of an irregular fragment with a dark rim along its edge, a smooth mid-grey interior and a few light streaks, against a light grey background at the top left. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Solid black background (#000000). Panels are flat #242424 with small corner radii (about 0.5% of width) and no shadow or border. Title is set in a wide-tracked medium-weight geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style) in white #FFFFFF, about 3.5% of slide height per line, centred. Column labels are bold, all caps, wide letter-spacing, about 1.7% of slide height, white #FFFFFF. Prices are medium-weight sans-serif with wide tracking, about 3.5% of slide height; the measurements give #FFFFFF for all four, but in the render the first three prices appear greyer (about #8C8C8C) and the Tesla price appears brighter white. Image boxes have small rounded corners and no shadow.

### Slide 44

**Text**

Instead, We Design For Expansion And Use / Raw Silicon To Increase Range 20% / RAW METALLURGICAL SILICON / STABILIZE SURFACE / THROUGH ELASTIC, / ION-CONDUCTING POLYMER COATING / ROBUST NETWORK / HIGHLY ELASTIC BINDER / + ELECTRODE DESIGN

**Layout**

Centered title block across the top, with a wide dark grey rounded panel spanning most of the slide width holding three columns left to right: raw silicon image, a vertical three-stage process column, and a network image, connected by horizontal arrows. Each column has a bold caption beneath the panel, centred under its visual. No footer or slide number is visible; margins are about 2.8% on the sides.

**Visuals**

Three-part left-to-right process diagram. (1) Photograph of a raw metallurgical silicon lump: a chunky, irregular, glossy silver-grey fragment with sharp facets and specular highlights, shown in black-and-white, full-colour-free, placed on the dark panel with no crop or overlay. (2) Vertical stack of three stages connected by thin light-grey downward arrows: top row shows three teardrop-shaped dark translucent droplets each containing four small pale blue-grey angular silicon particles, plus one more droplet below in the centre; middle row shows about nine smaller droplets arranged in two staggered rows (four on top, five below) each containing a pale blue-grey particle; bottom row shows about seven dark grey circular coated particles with pale blue-grey cores, arranged in a row of four above a row of three. (3) Illustration of a robust network: grey rounded blobby particles of varying size scattered in a horizontal band, linked by thin dark blue curved lines acting as binder strands, with a few small grey specks. Light grey (#9A9A9A approx.) horizontal arrows with open arrowheads sit between the first and second columns and between the second and third columns, pointing right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 background.

### Slide 45

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

37% / $/KWH REDUCTION / 14% / 18% / 5% / CATHODE / MATERIALS / CELL VEHICLE / INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Two-part composition on a black field: a large circular progress ring with a central figure sits in the left third, centred vertically (about x 7 to 40, y 21 to 79). A grid of five equal rounded cards occupies the right side: three cards in a top row (x 46.5 to 93.1, y 25.2 to 48.9) and two cards in a centred second row (x 54.5 to 85.1, y 51.1 to 74.8), each card 14.7 by 23.7 with a gap of about 1.3 between cards. No title, footer, page number or source line appears.

**Visuals**

Circular progress ring (shape): a thick, round-capped arc stroke forming a broken circle, about 1.3 percent of slide width in stroke width. Three bright white arc segments run clockwise from the upper-left gap across the top, down the right side and into the lower-right; two dark grey (#333333) track segments fill the left side and the lower-left, leaving gaps at the upper-left, lower-right and bottom. The ring encloses the figure 37% and its caption. Five cards: (1) top-left card, dark grey #242424, with a greyscale battery cell cylinder above the figure 14%; (2) top-middle card, #242424, with a greyscale battery module assembly with four protruding terminals above 18%; (3) top-right card, #242424, with a dark crumpled, foil-like lump above 5%; (4) bottom-left card, light grey #D5D5D5, with a mottled grey granular sphere above the label CATHODE MATERIALS in black; (5) bottom-right card, #242424, with a dimmed greyscale wireframe of a vehicle body frame above the dimmed label CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION. Images are dark, low-contrast renders on the cards with no visible lighting direction, not cropped, and not overlaid.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) primary text and white arcs. Card fills are #242424 except one light card at #D5D5D5. Track arcs are #333333. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like, GothamSSm). The large figure 37% is Medium weight; the card figures 14%, 18%, 5% are Bold. Captions are Bold, uppercase, letter-spaced. The large figure is about 9.4 percent of slide height, card figures about 3.3 percent, and captions about 1.7 percent. Cards have small corner radii (about 0.5 percent of slide width) and no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 46

**Text**

What Is A Battery Cathode? | CATHODES ARE LIKE BOOKSHELVES | METAL COST MATTERS | NICKEL / COBALT MANGANESE / ALUMINUM | LITHIUM | $/kWh METALS | 35 | 30 | 25 | 20 | 15 | 10 | 5 | 0 | COBALT | NICKEL | IRON | Wh/kg | 200 | 400 | 600 | 800 | 1000 | 1200 | 1400

**Layout**

Two equal-width panels sit side by side in the lower half of the slide, centred under two centred section headings, with the main title centred across the top. The left panel holds a crystal-structure illustration with two labels; the right panel holds a dot chart with a y-axis of $/kWh metals and an x-axis of Wh/kg. Margins are about 2.8% at the left and right edges. There is no footer and no slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Left: an illustration (not a photograph) of a battery cathode crystal structure. Four layers of translucent grey-white octahedra, each drawn as a flat slab with small white-grey spheres at its corners, stacked from top to bottom, with three intervening rows of lavender-grey spheres representing lithium between them. Each octahedral slab is slightly tilted. The illustration is set on the dark panel with no boxing and no overlay, and is not cropped. Two thin white leader arrows with open arrowheads: one from the label NICKEL / COBALT MANGANESE / ALUMINUM points right to the middle octahedral slab; one from the label LITHIUM points left to the lithium row beside it. Right: a scatter-style dot chart with no legend. Title printed as the panel's label: $/kWh METALS (vertical axis). Vertical axis: $/kWh, range 0 to 35, gridlines and labels at 0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30 and 35. Horizontal axis: Wh/kg, range about 200 to 1400, labels at 200, 400, 600, 800, 1000, 1200 and 1400. Three highlighted red points, each with a bold white label: COBALT at about 1080 Wh/kg and about 27.5 $/kWh; NICKEL at about 1150 Wh/kg and about 15 $/kWh; IRON at about 610 Wh/kg and about 4.5 $/kWh. Values are estimates marked 'about' because the slide prints no numeric values for the points.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Entirely dark, minimal look. Background pure black #000000. Panels dark charcoal #242424 with slightly rounded corners and no visible border or shadow. Title and section headings white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style, GothamSSm). Title is Medium weight, mixed case, with normal tracking, at about 3.5% of slide height. Section headings are Bold, all capitals, widely letter-spaced, at about 1.85% of slide height. Chart axis labels are Bold in grey #929292 at about 1.39%. Point labels COBALT, NICKEL and IRON are Bold white #FFFFFF at about 1.85%, with wide letter-spacing. Small illustration labels are Bold #A9A9A9 at about 1.11%, all capitals, letter-spaced. Accent colour is red #E8341C for the three chart points. Gridlines are thin grey #4A4A4A, about 0.1% of slide height. Body text is absent except labels.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Full-slide background fill, solid black (a white fill lies underneath it and is fully covered) (No border, no shadow, fully opaque)
- shape at x 2.8, y 28.2, w 46.6, h 49.6 [#242424]: Left panel, rounded rectangle backing the illustration (Corner radius about 0.6% of slide width, no border, no shadow, opacity 100%)
