### Slide 47

**Text**

High Nickel Cathode Development
MAXIMIZE NICKEL, REMOVE COBALT
NOVEL COATINGS & DOPANTS
15% REDUCTION IN CATHODE $/KWh

**Layout**

Centred title sits near the top of the slide, above a single horizontal row of three uppercase labels separated by two thin vertical dividers. Below them a wide greyscale micrograph spans nearly the full slide width inside side margins of about 1.8%, with empty black space beneath it. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One greyscale scanning electron micrograph of high-nickel cathode particles, boxed within a rectangle at x 1.8%, y 28.3%, w 94.4%, h 49.6% (1813 by 536 px) with slightly rounded corners. Upper left: a cluster of small rounded granules filling a rounded, partly cut-off particle. Lower left: a grainy, dark particle partly cropped by the left edge. Centre: a large curved spherical particle whose edge is outlined by a bright white rim running diagonally from top centre down to the bottom centre-left, with dark shadow to its left. Right half: densely packed faceted, blocky crystalline grains with flat faces and small bright specks at grain boundaries. Lighting is directional from the upper left, producing bright edge highlights and soft grey shading. Dominant colours are mid to light greys with black shadows. The image is cropped at the top, bottom and left edges of the box and is boxed rather than full-bleed. No overlay or tint. Two thin vertical grey divider rules sit between the labels, at about x 34.0% and x 66.0%, spanning y 22.5% to 25.4%.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Plain black slide with white type. The title is set in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style) in medium weight, sentence case, slightly letter-spaced, white #FFFFFF, at about 3.5% of slide height. The three labels are Gotham-style bold sans-serif, all caps, widely letter-spaced, white #FFFFFF, at about 1.85% of slide height. Dividers are thin grey #8C8C8C rules about 1 px wide. The micrograph has slightly rounded corners, no border and no shadow.

### Slide 48

**Text**

Diversified Cathode Approach
IRON BASED
LONG CYCLE LIFE
NICKEL + MANGANESE
LONG RANGE
HIGH NICKEL
MASS SENSITIVE

**Layout**

Three-column grid on a full-black slide. The title is centred near the top at about 10% from the top edge. Below it, a 5-column tile area sits in two rows: the left group holds a wide tile over two small tiles, the middle group holds a 2x2 grid of tiles, and the right group holds one tall tile. Each group has a centred caption pair below its tiles, separated by thin vertical divider lines. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Nine photographic product renders on dark grey tiles, all in white/silver with soft grey shadows on black. Left group: a small tile with a white cloth-covered car silhouette (covered vehicle, top left); a small tile with a silver Model 3 sedan in three-quarter view facing right and slightly down; a wide tile at the bottom with a white Megapack battery cabinet in three-quarter view, horizontal. Middle group: a tall-format tile with a white Tesla Powerwall wall battery, upright, with a TESLA wordmark in small grey letters near the top; a tile with a silver Model Y in three-quarter view facing right; a tile with a silver Model S sedan in three-quarter view facing right; a tile with a silver Model X in three-quarter view facing right. Right group: one large tile with a silver Cybertruck pickup in front and a silver Tesla Semi truck behind it, both in three-quarter view facing right, with soft grey shadow beneath. Lighting is soft studio light, neutral white and grey tones, no colour cast. The renders are full tiles, not cropped, with the product centred in each tile. Two thin vertical grey divider lines, about 0.2% wide and about 7% tall, separate the caption groups.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background #000000 with no texture. Titles are white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style, GothamSSm Medium), normal case, with slight letter-spacing. Captions are in GothamSSm Bold, all caps, letter-spaced, white #FFFFFF on the first line of each pair. The second caption line appears grey in the render, about #8C8C8C, although the file reports #FFFFFF for it. Tile fill #242424 with square-ish corners, roughly 0.5% radius, and no border. Title is about 3.5% of slide height and captions about 1.9%. Divider lines are light grey, about #9A9A9A, 1px. Tile gaps are about 1.3% of slide width.

**Layout**

A single centred horizontal composition on a black field with no grid of columns. The title sits near the top centre, a small uppercase caption sits centred above the bar, and a full-width segmented bar fills the middle band with category labels centred beneath each segment. The bar spans about 2.8% to 97.2% of the width, with the title and caption centred on the slide. There is no footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

A single 100% stacked horizontal bar chart with no axes, gridlines or legend, built as four rectangles of equal height (about 28.3% to 77.9% of slide height) with small rounded corners. Segments from left to right: Nickel 35% (grey #5E5E5E), Lithium 25% (dark grey, file measurement #5E5E5E, visually about #353535), Cobalt 5% (very dark grey, file measurement #5E5E5E, visually about #2A2A2A, the narrowest segment), and Processing 35% (red-orange #E53C24, highlighted). The Processing segment is the only highlighted point. Each segment carries its percentage label centred in white, and each has its category name centred in white uppercase below the bar. Segment widths are proportional to 35, 25, 5 and 35 percent of the bar.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Flat, minimal dark design on a pure black background (#000000). Titles and labels are white (#FFFFFF), with the single accent in red-orange (#E53C24) used only for the Processing segment. Grey segments are #5E5E5E and dark greys near #353535 and #2A2A2A. The typeface is a geometric sans-serif matching Gotham SSm: the title is Medium weight in sentence-like title case with generous letter-spacing, about 3.52% of slide height (about 3.5 on a 100 scale); the percentage numerals are Medium at about 4.63% of slide height; the uppercase captions and category labels are Bold at about 1.85% of slide height with wide letter-spacing. Bar segments have very small corner radii, about 0.5% of slide height, and no shadows or gradients. Thin dark seam strips cover the joints between segments.

### Slide 50

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Placing the traditional and Tesla cathode process side by side, with the same stages in the same order, makes the simplification easy to follow.
- Review: Each page carries one idea, with one accent color, one typeface and consistent titles, and the visuals do the explaining: the cell animation on slides 17 to 22, the wet versus dry electrode comparison on slides 32 and 34, and the blocks on slides 50 and 51, where the empty space in the dry-process column makes the point.
- Worth keeping: Keep the progress ring and tile tracker, the single red accent against black, the animated cell build on slides 17 to 22, and the sparse before and after process diagrams on slides 50 and 51.

**Text**

Traditional Cathode Process / METAL SULFATE PRODUCTION / RAW MATERIALS INPUT / CATHODE PRODUCTION / FINAL PRODUCT / METAL / SULPHURIC ACID / METAL SULFATE / MORE CHEMICALS / WATER / CATHODE / A BUNCH OF STUFF HAPPENS / WASTE WATER + BYPRODUCTS

**Layout**

Four vertical columns of boxed content span the slide below a centred title, with a thin vertical divider between each pair of adjacent column headers. Column headers sit in one row at about 23% of the slide height, and the box grid starts at about 28% and ends near 95%, leaving wide black margins left and right (about 3% and 2.5%). Text is centred within every box and column header; there is no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Abstract block diagram, no photographs or logos. Four columns of rectangles read as a process flow. Column 1 (Metal sulfate production): a grey #929292 bar labelled METAL at top, and below it a white box labelled SULPHURIC ACID. Column 2 (Raw materials input): a grey #929292 box labelled METAL SULFATE, a short white box labelled MORE CHEMICALS beneath it, and a tall white box labelled WATER filling the rest of the column. Column 3 (Cathode production): one tall red #E53C24 box labelled A BUNCH OF STUFF HAPPENS spanning the full column height. Column 4 (Final product): a red #E53C24 box labelled CATHODE at top, and below it a tall white box labelled WASTE WATER + BYPRODUCTS. No arrows or connecting lines are drawn between boxes; the vertical divider lines are the only linear elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

### Slide 51

**Review notes**

- Review: Each page carries one idea, with one accent color, one typeface and consistent titles, and the visuals do the explaining: the cell animation on slides 17 to 22, the wet versus dry electrode comparison on slides 32 and 34, and the blocks on slides 50 and 51, where the empty space in the dry-process column makes the point.
- Worth keeping: Keep the progress ring and tile tracker, the single red accent against black, the animated cell build on slides 17 to 22, and the sparse before and after process diagrams on slides 50 and 51.

**Text**

Tesla Cathode / METAL SULFATE PRODUCTION / RAW MATERIALS INPUT / CATHODE PRODUCTION / FINAL PRODUCT / METAL / SULPHURIC ACID / METAL / MORE CHEMICAL / WATER / WATER / LESS STUFF HAPPENS / CATHODE / WASTE WATER / + BYPRODUCTS

**Layout**

Four equal-height columns of blocks sit beneath a centred title, with column headers aligned on a single horizontal line near 23% of the slide height. Thin vertical white rules separate the headers at roughly 26%, 50% and 74% of the width. Margins are about 3% left and right; the blocks run from about 28% down to about 95% of the height. No footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Diagram of stacked rectangular blocks, not a chart. Column 1 'METAL SULFATE PRODUCTION': two dark grey blocks stacked at top, labelled METAL (short block) and SULPHURIC ACID (taller block); the rest of the column is empty black. Column 2 'RAW MATERIALS INPUT': a stack of four blocks from top to bottom, labelled METAL (mid-grey #929292 top bar with dark block below it), MORE CHEMICAL (dark block), WATER (thin white bar), and a tall dark block labelled WATER filling the rest of the column to the bottom. Column 3 'CATHODE PRODUCTION': one tall dark block with a red top block labelled LESS STUFF HAPPENS covering about the top 40% of the column. Column 4 'FINAL PRODUCT': a red block labelled CATHODE at top, then a tall dark block labelled WASTE WATER / + BYPRODUCTS filling the rest. No arrows, lines connecting the columns, logos or icons; the only lines are the thin column dividers.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. Title 'Tesla Cathode' in white (#FFFFFF) Gotham-style geometric sans-serif, medium weight, mixed case, letter-spaced slightly, about 3.5% of slide height. Column headers in white bold sans-serif (Gotham Bold), all capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 1.85% of slide height. Block labels in bold all-caps sans-serif with wide letter-spacing, about 1.85% of slide height; labels on dark blocks are black on #242424, labels on red or white blocks are white or black as listed. Accent red #E53C24 for the LESS STUFF HAPPENS and CATHODE blocks. Dark panels #242424, mid-grey #929292 for the top METAL bar in column 2, white #FFFFFF bar for WATER. Blocks are plain rectangles with square corners, no shadows, no outlines. Dividers are thin white vertical lines.

**Layout**

Three equal-width statistic columns sit across the top third of a black slide, each with a large white figure above a small grey all-caps label, centred on the column. A single wide photograph spans nearly the full slide width below them, with a left and right margin of about 2.8% and no title, footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

One photograph of an industrial process line: a long row of white free-standing equipment cabinets (about 17 units) with perforated white side panels at each end, running left to right across the frame. Above the cabinets run silver stainless steel pipes with elbows and vertical risers, and red safety railings and red-painted structure sit at the upper right. A grey vertical steel column stands in front of the cabinets near the centre. Small grey-and-white warning labels and red indicator tabs are on the cabinet fronts. The floor is pale grey concrete. Lighting is even, flat and bright from above, with a neutral white-grey palette and red accents. The image is a rectangle with small rounded corners, not cropped to full bleed, boxed inside the black background. No overlay or tint. Statistic figures are not charted.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Solid black background with white figures and grey labels. Figures are set in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style) medium weight at about 8.2% of slide height, white #FFFFFF. Labels are a bold geometric sans-serif in all caps with wide letter-spacing at about 1.7% of slide height, grey #929292. Photograph has small rounded corners and no border or shadow.

### Slide 54

**Text**

North American Cathode Production / BUILD PLANT / SHORTEN TRAVEL DISTANCE / Ni / Li / TESLA / CATHODE / LITHIUM / CLAYS / Ni / Li / Ni / Li / KG x KM / CATHODE / LiOH-H2O / Li SPODUMENE / NICKEL POWDER / 30000 / 25000 / 20000 / 15000 / 10000 / 5000 / 0 / Status Quo / Localized Supply Chain

**Layout**

Centred title sits at the top (about 10% from the top edge). Two centred uppercase column headings sit at about 23% height, one above each of two side-by-side rounded dark panels that span about 2.7% to 97.3% of the width and about 28% to 78% of the height: a map panel on the left and a bar chart panel on the right. Both panels share the same top and bottom edges. There is no footer, no source line and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Two visuals. (1) Left panel: a black, flat, outline-only map of the contiguous United States with white-grey state borders, occupying about x 7.3 to 44.6 and y 32.9 to 72.9 of the slide. Overlaid on it are: a dark grey #5A5A5A circle labelled LITHIUM / CLAYS over the Nevada/California area; a 3D rendered isometric block of Tesla cathode material (pale blue-silver, slab-shaped, with light reflections on its top faces) over the Oklahoma/Kansas area with the caption TESLA / CATHODE above it; and six circular badges: two light grey Ni badges and two dark grey Li badges in the north-east (Ni at upper left of pair, Li beside it), one Li badge over North Carolina, one Ni badge and one Li badge stacked over the Gulf coast of Texas and Louisiana. (2) Right panel: a vertical stacked bar chart titled KG x KM with a y-axis from 0 to 30000 (gridlines and labels at 0, 5000, 10000, 15000, 20000, 25000, 30000, no axis unit beyond KG x KM) and a categorical x-axis with two categories, Status Quo and Localized Supply Chain. Status Quo bar: about 28400 total, made of a dark bottom segment about 0 to 2200, a mid grey segment about 2200 to 22900, a thin light grey band about 22900 to 23400 and a white top segment about 23400 to 28400 (values are estimates, no values are printed). Localized Supply Chain bar: about 5650 total, a dark bottom segment about 0 to 830 and a mid grey segment about 830 to 5650 (estimates). Legend: four pill-shaped toggles in a row at the top right of the chart, CATHODE (white pill, highlighted), LiOH-H2O (light grey pill), Li SPODUMENE (mid grey pill), NICKEL POWDER (dark grey pill). Highlighted point: the white CATHODE pill and the white top segment of the Status Quo bar are the brightest elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Pure black slide with white and grey type in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style, GothamSSm). Title is Medium weight, white, about 3.5% of slide height, with slightly open letter-spacing. Column headings are Bold, uppercase, white, about 1.85% of slide height, wide letter-spacing. Chart and map labels are Bold uppercase white about 1.1 to 1.4% of slide height; axis values and category labels are Bold mixed-case or numeric in grey #929292 at about 1.39%. Map badge letters are Medium at about 1.67%. Panels are flat #242424 rounded rectangles with small corner radii (about 1% of slide width), no shadows, no borders. Bar segments have flat fills with no gradients; horizontal gridlines are thin grey (about #3A3A3A to #5A5A5A, 1 px) across the chart width. Legend pills are fully rounded capsules with bold uppercase labels about 0.93% of slide height.

**Layout**

Single slide with a centred title at the top and two equal side-by-side rounded panels below, each with a centred letter-spaced caption above it. The left panel holds a map of North America, the right panel holds two stacked statistics separated by a thin horizontal rule. The panels share a common top and bottom edge with side margins of about 2.7 percent. No footer, logo or slide number is present.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a black-on-dark-grey map of the contiguous United States outline with thin dark-grey state borders. Two Tesla battery-storage style 3D isometric blocks (light grey-white, rendered) sit over the central plains, labelled TESLA CATHODE and TESLA LITHIUM. Map markers: a large grey circle labelled LITHIUM CLAYS over the west coast area; small circular badges reading Ni (light circle with grey text) and Li (dark grey circle with white text) placed over the north-east, south-east coast, and Gulf coast. Right panel: no chart; two large numerals, 33% above REDUCTION IN LITHIUM COST and 100% above ELECTRIC FACILITY, with a thin grey horizontal rule between them.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background. Titles and numerals white (#FFFFFF). Captions in light grey (#929292). Panel fill #242424 with small corner radius. Map state fills #000000 with dark grey borders. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham (Medium for title and numerals, Bold for captions, letter-spaced capitals). Title about 3.5 percent of slide height; numerals about 8.2 percent; captions about 1.7 percent; map labels about 1.1 to 1.2 percent. Flat style with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 56

**Text**

Access Even More Lithium
SIGNIFICANT UNTAPPED LITHIUM IN CLAY
TESLA ACID-FREE, SALINE EXTRACTION
TESLA SECURED TWH-SCALE RESOURCE
ENOUGH LITHIUM IN NEVADA TO ELECTRIFY THE ENTIRE U.S. FLEET

**Layout**

Centered title in the upper part of the slide, followed by a single horizontal row of three uppercase statements separated by two thin vertical rules. A wide photograph fills the middle band between margins of about 2.8% on the left and right, with a centered uppercase caption beneath it. No footer, logo or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a wide panoramic view of an arid Nevada desert landscape under a clear blue sky. Low, dark brown-grey mountain ridges run across the horizon at about 33% to 40% of the image height from the top. In the foreground are pale sandy and grey-beige hillsides, dry scrub and sparse shrubs, with a pale eroded rock outcrop near the centre-right. The photo is a stitched panorama with faint seams visible in the sky. Lighting is bright, even daylight. Dominant colours are deep blue sky (#1F4E8C at top, fading to about #7FA3C8 near the horizon), tan and beige ground (#B9A88A), and dark brown ridges (#4A4038). The image is not cropped, is boxed with slightly rounded corners, and has no overlay or tint. Two thin vertical grey rules sit between the three statements: one at about x 34.1%, y 22.5%, the other at about x 66.0%, y 22.5%, each about 3.0% tall and light grey (#8A8A8A).

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Solid black slide with white text throughout. The title is a geometric sans-serif in Gotham Medium, sentence case, with slight letter-spacing, at about 3.5% of slide height. The three statements and the caption are Gotham Bold in all capitals with generous letter-spacing, at about 1.85% of slide height (cap height about 1.3%). Title color #FFFFFF, body and label text #FFFFFF, rule accent #8A8A8A. The photograph has small corner radii of about 0.5% of slide height and no shadow. No gradients.

### Slide 57

**Text**

Recycling Elements From Cells Is Far More Desirable Than From Raw Ores / SOURCE / NICKEL / LITHIUM / COBALT / RECYCLED CELLS / 20% / 2.7% / 2% / TYPICAL ORES / 1.2% / 0.7% / 0.2%

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred title across the top, followed by a four-column table on the left and two stacked photographs on the right. The table has a header row of spaced caps labels above a thin horizontal rule, then two data rows separated by a second thin rule. Column headers and values are centred within their columns, with the photographs in a right-hand column sharing each row's height. Margins are about 3% on the left and right. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

A two-row comparison table with a photograph beside each row. Columns: SOURCE (row labels), NICKEL, LITHIUM, COBALT, and an unlabelled photo column. Row 1, RECYCLED CELLS: NICKEL 20%, LITHIUM 2.7%, COBALT 2%, in large white numerals. Row 2, TYPICAL ORES: NICKEL 1.2%, LITHIUM 0.7%, COBALT 0.2%, in large mid-grey numerals (#929292), so the recycled row is emphasised by its brighter white colour. Photo 1 (top right, about 66.6% to 97.2% x, 28.3% to 60.6% y): a close-up, top-down view of shredded battery-cell scrap, dark grey and black flakes with metallic silver shards and scattered copper-red fragments, filling the frame edge to edge; lighting is flat and even; the frame is cropped tightly with no visible edges; slightly rounded corners; boxed, not full-bleed. Photo 2 (bottom right, about 66.6% to 97.2% x, 62.8% to 95.1% y): a close-up of a rough mineral ore surface with blue-grey and pale white crusted patches and rust-brown weathered areas, textured and irregular, filling the frame; lighting is flat and even; slightly rounded corners; boxed, not full-bleed. Rules: two thin dark-grey hairlines (about #2A2A2A, roughly 1 px) spanning about 2.8% to 97.2% of the width, one under the header row at about 27.1% y and one between the data rows at about 61.7% y.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) titles, header labels and recycled values, and mid-grey (#929292) for the typical-ores values and label. There is no accent colour; the imagery supplies the only colour. Typefaces are Gotham SSm, a geometric sans-serif: Medium for the title and numerals, Bold in all caps with wide letter-spacing for the column headers and row labels. The title is about 3.5% of slide height, with a generous letter-spacing; the numerals are about 5.9% of slide height; the header and label caps are about 1.9% of slide height. Lines are thin hairlines in dark grey. Photos have small corner radii (about 0.3% of width) with no shadows, gradients or borders.

### Slide 59

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

49%
$/KWH REDUCTION
14%
18%
5%
12%
CELL VEHICLE
INTEGRATION

**Layout**

Two-part composition on a black field. A circular gauge sits in the left half, centred at about x 24%, y 50%. A 3-over-2 grid of five square-cornered cards sits in the right half, with the top row of three starting at x 46.5% and the bottom row of two offset to x 54.5%. There is no title, footer, or slide number. Margins are roughly 7% on the left and 6% on the right.

**Visuals**

Circular progress gauge: a ring of thick white arcs with round caps, drawn over a black background. The ring spans about x 7% to 40% and y 21% to 79%. Roughly three-quarters of the ring is white. The upper-left segment, running from about 10 o'clock to 8 o'clock, is dark grey (#333333). Small gaps sit at about 11 o'clock, 2 to 3 o'clock, 5 o'clock and 7 o'clock. The gauge centre carries the large figure 49% and the caption $/KWH REDUCTION. Five cards: (1) 14%, showing a grey cylindrical battery cell, upright, dark metallic with a soft highlight. (2) 18%, showing a grey moulded plastic battery-module housing with cylindrical cells and connectors, shown from an oblique angle. (3) 5%, showing a crumpled, folded silver-grey foil shape. (4) 12%, showing a textured dark grey sphere, grainy, roughly 1:1. (5) CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION, showing a light grey-silver wireframe of a car body frame and floor pan in side-angled view, with the caption in black beneath. Each image is centred in its card, with the percentage below it. All pictures are isolated objects on flat fills, not cropped or boxed by photography. No overlays or tints.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background with a white ring gauge, white bold sans-serif numerals and grey-on-black card tiles. Titles and figures are white (#FFFFFF). The caption $/KWH REDUCTION is set in small caps-style letterspaced grey. The light card's caption is black (#000000). Typeface is Gotham (GothamSSm), a geometric sans-serif. The large 49% uses Gotham Medium, about 9.4% of slide height. Card figures and captions use Gotham Bold, about 3.3% of slide height for the percentages and about 1.7% for the caption text. Ring stroke is about 2% of slide height, white (#FFFFFF), with round caps; the dark arc is #333333. Cards are flat fills with square or very slightly rounded corners (about 0.3% radius), no shadows, no gradients. The highlighted fifth card is solid light grey #D5D5D5.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Background fill covering the full slide (Full-bleed flat fill; no gradient or pattern)

### Slide 61

**Text**

Giga Casting Innovations
SHOT SIZE ∝ VELOCITY ∝ PRESSURE ∝ TONNAGE
NEW ALLOY, NO HEAT TREATING OR COATINGS

**Layout**

Single centred column on a full black slide. The title sits centred at the top (about 10% from the top edge), with a row of two small caps labels beneath it: the left label starts at about 9.8% from the left edge and the right label is right-aligned to about 89% of the width. A wide dark grey panel spans the width between about 2.7% and 97.3% margins from the top of the labels row down to about 78% of the height, with the simulation image centred inside it. There is no footer, no slide number and no logo.

**Visuals**

One photograph-like rendered image (a computer simulation render, not a photograph) of a single large cast automotive underbody or structural part, shown as a 3D model at an oblique angle. The part is a curved, ribbed casting with an open window-like cutout on the right side and lattice-like ribs; it is rotated so its long axis runs diagonally from lower left to upper right. Colouring is a heat-map style gradient: mostly yellow-orange across the body, green along the upper and lower edges and flanges, and a saturated red blob with orange gradient streaks at the lower-left inlet end, with small blue marks at the red region's edge. The part is lit evenly with soft shading and no visible light source; the background behind it is a flat dark grey (#191919). Composition is centred, unbordered inside the panel, not cropped, and the render is not full-bleed. The image is 1108 by 624 pixels, placed at about 21.1% to 78.8% of the width and 24.5% to 82.3% of the height, sitting on top of the grey panel with no overlay or tint. No axes, legend or numeric values are shown.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 full-slide background. Pure black slide with a dark grey (#191919) rounded rectangle panel behind the image; the panel has very small corner radii (about 0.5% of slide height) and no visible border or shadow. Title is white (#FFFFFF) in a geometric sans-serif, Gotham SSm Medium (or the closest common geometric sans such as Montserrat Medium), mixed case, slight positive letter-spacing, about 3.5% of slide height, with lowercase letters about 0.7 of cap height. The two labels and the right statement are white (#FFFFFF) in Gotham SSm Bold (closest common: Montserrat Bold), all capitals, wide letter-spacing, about 1.85% of slide height. The ∝ symbols between the labels are set in a symbol font (Apple Symbols) in white at about 3.7% of slide height. Body text is the only text level; there are no accent colours on the slide apart from the warm and green colours inside the render.

### Slide 62

**Text**

Structural Fuel Tanks Improve Mass And Range
EARLY / AIRCRAFT WING
STORAGE
EXCESS STRUCTURE
MODERN / AIRCRAFT WING
STORAGE

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred title near the top; two horizontal rows below, each with a narrow dark-grey label panel on the left (about 2.8% to 17.5% of width) and a wide dark-grey image panel on the right (about 18.7% to 97.2% of width), separated by gutters of about 1% of width. The top row sits at y 28.3% to 60.7% and the bottom row at y 62.8% to 95.1%. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Two cross-section diagrams of aircraft wings in light grey (#C8C8C8 approximately) on dark grey panels. Top: Early aircraft wing, a thick airfoil profile with a leading edge at left and a long tapering trailing edge at right; internal structure consists of a front spar, a rear spar and several triangular and rectangular cut-out ribs/bays appearing as black voids. Within the central bay is a blue (#3F6FA0 approximately) filled shape representing fuel storage, outlined by a red (#D00000 approximately) stroke that marks excess structure surrounding the tank. Bottom: Modern aircraft wing, a slimmer airfoil cross-section with a large blue (#3F6FA0 approximately) central fuel tank filling most of the interior between grey structural bulkheads, with small semicircular notches along the tank edge, and grey end sections containing black circular lightening holes of decreasing size toward the trailing edge. Two blue pill-shaped labels sit at right of the top panel: STORAGE, and a red pill labelled EXCESS STRUCTURE; one blue pill STORAGE sits at right of the bottom panel. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

### Slide 63

**Text**

Structural Batteries Improve Mass And Range / MODERN / AUTOMOTIVE / BATTERY / STORAGE / EXCESS STRUCTURE / FUTURE / AUTOMOTIVE / BATTERY / STORAGE

**Layout**

Two stacked horizontal rows, each with a narrow dark label panel on the left (about 15% of width) and a wide dark image panel on the right (about 79% of width), separated by a gap of about 1.5% of width. The title is centred horizontally near the top at about 10% from the top edge. Left margin is about 2.8% and right margin about 2.7%. The STORAGE and EXCESS STRUCTURE tags sit at the right edge inside the top image panel, and a STORAGE tag sits at the right edge inside the lower image panel. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Two cross-section diagrams of a vehicle underbody battery, each shown as a photographic render. Top (Modern Automotive Battery): grey metallic side pillars at both ends, a wide light-grey cross beam spanning the top of the pack, and a dense double row of blue cylindrical cells (about 60 visible, in several clusters separated by red cross members) inside a tray. Red outlined shapes mark excess structure: end-cap ribs on both left and right sides and red ribs running along the tray and between cell groups. Lower (Future Automotive Battery): the same grey side pillars and cross beam, with a single row of about 25 blue cylindrical cells in a shallow tray with dark cell bases beneath, and no red highlighting. Lighting is even and studio-like, with a black background showing through and metallic grey highlights on the structure. Both images are unframed, not cropped, not full-bleed, and sit directly on the dark panels with no overlay or tint. Blue cells are about #4A7FB5 and red outlines are about #D10000. Tags: STORAGE is a blue rounded pill (about #41729F) with white text, and EXCESS STRUCTURE is a red rounded pill (about #D00000) with white text.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 2.8, y 28.3, w 14.7, h 32.4 [#191919]: Left label panel, top row, rounded rectangle (Flat fill #191919, corner radius about 0.6% of slide width, no border, no shadow.)

### Slide 64

**Text**

Revolution In Body + Battery Engineering
10% MASS REDUCTION
14% RANGE INCREASE OPPORTUNITY
370 FEWER PARTS

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred title across the top, a single row of three left-aligned statistics beneath it separated by two thin vertical rules, and a large centred product image filling the lower two-thirds. No footer, logo or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a metallic vehicle underbody (body and battery structure) isolated on black. Subject: a silver-grey aluminium floor and battery frame viewed at an angle from the front-left, with a long central floor section, a raised battery tray in the rear-centre-right, cast strut towers at each end and a wheel-arch or crash structure at the far right. Composition: a wide, roughly horizontal object centred in the frame, cut out from its background with no border, not cropped, not boxed, no overlay or tint. Lighting: directional studio light producing bright specular highlights on the metal and dark grey recesses. Dominant colours: silver-grey, light grey highlights and near-black shadows against #000000. Two thin vertical grey divider rules sit between the three statistics.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Black background (#000000) with white text throughout. Title in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham SSm Medium), sentence case, white #FFFFFF, about 3.5% of slide height for the cap-height line, with slight tracking. Statistics in Gotham SSm Bold, all capitals, widely letter-spaced, white #FFFFFF, about 1.85% of slide height. Dividers are thin hairlines in mid grey #8C8C8C, about 0.1% of slide width wide. No shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients; the only accent is the grey rules.

Centred title across the top band, then two equal-width columns with matching margins of about 2.7% on each side. No footer, page number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Two black-and-white technical floor-plan drawings, shown as thin white CAD-style line work on black. Left drawing (current): a wide, dense plan made of many rectangular machine and cell outlines arranged in a long horizontal band with vertical blocks and a dense cluster at the left; it spans nearly the full width of its card and is the larger of the two. Right drawing (future): a smaller, more compact plan with fewer blocks, a long horizontal conveyor-like band along its lower edge, and a few blocks tinted orange, red, blue and green-yellow; it is centred in its card with wide empty margins. Both drawings are unframed line art on the dark card, not photographs; no axes, legend or values appear. A full-width low-contrast image strip sits behind the top of the slide, appearing as a faint dark gradient with no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

### Slide 66

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.

**Text**

56% / $/KWH REDUCTION / 14% / 18% / 5% / 12% / 7%

**Layout**

Two-part composition on a black field. A large broken white ring with the headline figure 56% and its caption sits centred in the left half (ring centre about x 23.7%, y 50%). On the right, five dark grey rounded cards are arranged in two staggered rows: three across the top (starting x 46.5%, y 25.2%) and two below (starting x 54.5%, y 51.1%), with the second row offset by half a card. There is no title, footer, slide number or logo; margins are roughly 7% left, 6% right and 21% top and bottom.

**Visuals**

Shape: a broken ring of five white arcs with round caps, drawn as a donut-like gauge around the 56% figure; the gaps sit at roughly the upper-left, upper-right, lower-right, bottom and left positions. Five cards: each is a flat #242424 rounded rectangle holding one grey, monochrome, dimmed image and one bold white percentage. Top row, left to right: a cylindrical battery cell (about 14%), a stacked motor or drive-unit assembly with cylindrical housings and connectors (18%), and a crumpled, folded sheet of foil-like material (5%). Bottom row, left to right: a rough, granular spherical lump with a textured surface (12%), and an open vehicle frame or chassis with roll-cage bars and a floor tray (7%). All images are greyscale with low contrast against the card, unboxed inside their cards and not cropped. A 393 by 299 px image is measured at x 44.0%, y 21.3%, but it is not visibly distinct on the slide. No chart axes or legend; the ring is a decorative gauge with no numeric scale printed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with no gradient or texture. Headline figure '56%' and percentage labels '14%', '18%', '5%', '12%', '7%' are white #FFFFFF. Caption '$/KWH REDUCTION' is tracked uppercase, set in white #FFFFFF (appears lighter grey on screen). Typeface: geometric sans-serif in the Gotham family (GothamSSm), Medium for the 56% figure and Bold for the small labels. Size: the 56% figure is about 9.4% of slide height (cap height about 6.6%); percentage labels about 3.3%; caption about 1.7%. Ring strokes are white #FFFFFF, about 2% of slide height thick, with rounded caps. Cards are #242424 with rounded corners of about 0.5% of slide width, no border, no shadow and no gradient.

### Slide 67

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A stacked breakdown of cost reductions by component, shown in several small charts, gives the argument for vertical integration in one place.
- Review: The deck makes a long technical argument easy to follow: climate framing in slides 2 to 5, two goals on slides 6 to 11, a five-part plan on slide 15, and then a repeating progress ring (slides 16, 29, 40, 45, 59, 66) that shows the running cost-per-kWh reduction and pays off in the stacked benefits on slide 67.
- Review: Small caption text on some diagrams (slides 6, 67) is only fit for a large screen, and a few pages carry little content beyond a photo and a stat, but this suits a live keynote with narration.
- What would change the score: I would label the key values on the line charts (slides 10, 11, 68) and make the slide 67 waterfall bars carry more of the color emphasis so the totals read faster.

**Text**

Stacking Up The Benefits Of Tesla’s Vertical Integration / RANGE INCREASE / $ / KWH REDUCTION / INVESTMENT PER GWH REDUCTION / CELL VEHICLE / INTEGRATION / 14% / 4% / CATHODE / MATERIAL / 20% / ANODE / MATERIAL / 16% / CELL DESIGN / 54% / 7% / 12% / 5% / 18% / 14% / CELL / FACTORY / ANODE / MATERIAL / CATHODE / MATERIAL / CELL DESIGN / 56% / CELL VEHICLE / INTEGRATION / 8% / 16% / 4% / 34% / 7% / CATHODE / MATERIAL / ANODE / MATERIAL / CELL / FACTORY / CELL DESIGN / 69%

**Layout**

Three equal columns, each a dark rounded-corner panel with a bold letter-spaced column heading above it, all sitting on a black slide. The slide title is centred across the top at about 10% of the height. Each panel holds a stepped waterfall-style bar diagram that rises from left to right, with a red vertical bracket and a large red percentage at its right side. Margins are about 3% at the sides; there is no footer, logo or slide number visible.

**Visuals**

Three stepped bar diagrams (waterfall style), one per panel, no axes, no numeric scale, no legend. Bars are mid-grey #5E5E5E rectangles with white bold percentage labels inside; grey category labels (#929292) sit beside or below each bar; thin grey connector lines join the bar steps. Left panel RANGE INCREASE: CELL DESIGN 16%, ANODE MATERIAL 20%, CATHODE MATERIAL 4%, CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION 14%, total 54% in red. Middle panel $ / KWH REDUCTION: CELL DESIGN 14%, CELL FACTORY 18%, ANODE MATERIAL 5%, CATHODE MATERIAL 12%, CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION 7%, total 56% in red. Right panel INVESTMENT PER GWH REDUCTION: CELL DESIGN 7%, CELL FACTORY 34%, ANODE MATERIAL 4%, CATHODE MATERIAL 16%, CELL VEHICLE INTEGRATION 8%, total 69% in red. Each panel has a red (#E53C24) vertical bracket line with small horizontal end ticks on its right side, spanning from the top step to the bottom baseline, with the percentage total set on the bracket's midpoint. No highlighted points, no photographs, no logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black #000000 slide background with dark charcoal #242424 panels, each with rounded corners of about 1% of slide width and no visible shadow. Bars #5E5E5E with no outline. Title and column headings white #FFFFFF in GothamSSm Medium (title) and GothamSSm Bold (headings), sans-serif, letter-spaced and uppercase for headings; title about 3.5% of slide height, headings about 1.9%. Bar percentage labels white #FFFFFF GothamSSm Bold about 1.7%. Category labels #929292 GothamSSm Bold uppercase, letter-spaced, about 1.3%. Totals and brackets in red #E53C24 GothamSSm Bold about 3.5% for totals; bracket lines about 0.3% of slide width thick. Connector lines thin grey.

### Slide 68

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would label the key values on the line charts (slides 10, 11, 68) and make the slide 67 waterfall bars carry more of the color emphasis so the totals read faster.

**Text**

A New Trajectory
$/kWh
INDUSTRY TREND
FORECAST
TESLA
0
2012
2013
2014
2015
2016
2017
2018

2020
2021

2023

2025

**Layout**

A single centred title sits near the top of the slide at about 10% from the top, with a full-width line chart below it spanning roughly 3% to 97% horizontally. The legend pills are right-aligned on the same row as the '$/kWh' unit label, which is left-aligned at the chart's top-left edge. Horizontal gridlines run edge to edge across the plot, and year labels sit in a single row beneath the x-axis baseline. There is no footer, slide number, logo or source line.

**Visuals**

Line chart, untitled apart from the slide title 'A New Trajectory'. Y-axis: '$/kWh', the only printed y-value is '0' at the baseline; twelve horizontal gridlines are evenly spaced above it with no numeric labels, so values are not printed. X-axis: calendar years 2012 to 2025, one label per year, with the 2012 label at the left and 2025 at the right. Series 1, INDUSTRY TREND: solid mid-grey line (about #6E6E73), starting at about 2013 near the top gridline and falling steeply to about 2014, then more gently through 2016, 2018 and 2020, ending at about 2021 at roughly 40% of the plot height above the baseline. Series 2, FORECAST: dashed light grey-white line (about #D9D9D9) with long dashes, starting at the end of the industry trend near 2021 and running to about 2025, declining only slightly. Series 3, TESLA: solid red line (about #E63B24), beginning at about 2022 on the forecast line, falling more steeply through 2023 and 2024 and ending at about 2025 well below the forecast line. No values are printed on any series, so all positions are estimates. Legend: three rounded pill labels at top right: INDUSTRY TREND on a grey pill (about #66666E) with white text, FORECAST on a light grey pill (about #D4D4D4) with black text, TESLA on a red pill (about #E63B24) with white text. No highlighted points or markers.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background with white and grey type. Title is white, medium-weight geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like), letter-spaced slightly, with mixed case. Unit label and legend labels are bold white or black sans-serif in capitals with letter-spacing. Year labels are medium grey (#929292) sans-serif. Gridlines are thin dark grey (about #2A2A2A), 1 px hairlines. Line weights: industry trend and Tesla about 3 px, forecast dashed about 3 px. Legend pills are fully rounded (pill-shaped) with no shadow and no gradients.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#000000]: Full-slide background rectangle (Solid fill covering the entire slide; a white #FFFFFF fill sits beneath it in the file but is fully hidden.)
- line at x 2.7, y 31.6, w 94.5, h 51.2 [#2A2A2A]: Horizontal gridlines, twelve, evenly spaced (1 px hairlines spanning from about x 2.7% to 97.2%, at about y 31.6, 36.4, 41.0, 45.7, 50.3, 55.0, 59.6, 64.3, 69.0, 73.6, 78.3 and 82.8; the lowest gridline at 82.8 is the zero baseline.)
- line at x 14.8, y 31.8, w 51.3, h 26.9 [#6E6E73]: INDUSTRY TREND series, solid grey curve from about 2013 to about 2021 (About 3 px stroke, smooth decaying curve, no markers; overlaps the forecast line at its right end near 2021.)
- line at x 66.2, y 58.7, w 26.6, h 4 [#D9D9D9]: FORECAST series, dashed curve from about 2021 to about 2025 (About 3 px dashed stroke with long dashes, slightly declining; begins where the industry trend ends and ends at about 2025.)
- line at x 70.8, y 59.4, w 22.5, h 13 [#E63B24]: TESLA series, solid red curve from about 2022 to about 2025 (About 3 px solid stroke, smooth, steepening between 2022 and 2024, flattening slightly toward 2025; starts on the forecast line.)

### Slide 69

**Text**

Help Us Accelerate The Transition / CELL EQUIPMENT / MANUFACTURERS / NEW AND EXISTING / CELL SUPPLIERS / RAW MATERIAL / MANUFACTURERS / MINERS AND REFINERS

**Layout**

Title is centred horizontally in the top band, about 10% from the top. Below it a 2x2 grid of four equal-sized rounded photo tiles fills the lower two-thirds, with a margin of about 2.8% on the left and right and gaps of about 4% horizontally and 2% vertically. Each tile carries its label in the lower-left area, left-aligned, except the miners tile whose label sits at mid-height. There is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in a 2x2 grid. Top-left: a long white-walled cell-equipment production line receding toward the viewer, with blurred workers in dark clothing walking on a grey floor and a red sign in the background; cool, bright, overexposed white light; full-bleed inside a rounded tile; a dark grey gradient overlay darkens the left side. Top-right: a close view of a worker in a white hard hat with CM lettering and a black face mask and high-visibility vest, leaning over a metal bench beside automated cell-assembly machinery; warm neutral grey tones with a dark overlay on the left. Bottom-left: a worker in a white cleanroom suit walking across a pale blue floor between large stainless steel tanks, pipes and control panels; cool blue-grey palette; a dark overlay on the left. Bottom-right: a molten-metal pour with a large column of orange-yellow flame and sparks from a furnace spout, a worker in a silver heat-resistant suit and hard hat at right; dark, near-black surroundings with orange highlights; the tile is darkened overall. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black #000000 full-slide background with no gradient. Title and tile labels are white #FFFFFF. Title is a geometric sans-serif (Gotham Medium), mixed case, with wide letter-spacing, about 3.5% of slide height. Labels are Gotham Bold capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 1.7% of slide height. Photo tiles have small rounded corners (about 0.3% of slide height), no borders and no shadows; each has a semi-transparent dark gradient darkening the left or bottom edge so white labels remain legible.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 71

**Text**

Electric Vehicle Powertrains That Cost Less
Than Combustion Engines
$25,000 PASSENGER CAR

**Layout**

Centered single-column layout on a black field. Two-line title sits at the top centre above a wide dark grey panel that spans nearly the full slide width (margins about 2.7% each side). A centred caption sits below the panel; the lower part of the slide is empty black.

**Visuals**

One photograph-style rendering of a car covered by a white/light grey fabric dust cover, shown in three-quarter view from the front-left, sitting on a dark grey panel. The cover drapes over the body with folds; the wheels are barely visible under the fabric at the left and right ends, with a soft shadow beneath. Lighting is soft and diffuse from above, producing gentle highlights on the fabric folds. Dominant colours: near-white fabric (#EDEDED to #F5F5F5) on dark grey (#242424) and black (#000000). The image is boxed inside the grey panel, not full-bleed, with no overlay or tint and no cropping of the car, which sits centred in the panel with generous empty space on all sides.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Black background (#000000) with a dark grey panel (#242424) having slightly rounded corners of about 0.5% of slide width. Title and caption in white (#FFFFFF). Title is a geometric sans-serif, GothamSSm Medium, sentence-case-style title case with slight letter-spacing, about 3.5% of slide height per line, with line spacing about 4.2% of slide height. Caption is GothamSSm Bold, all capitals, letter-spaced, about 1.85% of slide height. No accent colours, no shadows on text, no gradients, no borders.
